# Habit Tracker — DayStamp (App Store ID 1456241316) — Full Review Analysis

**App:** Habit Tracker - DayStamp — *Daily Routine, Streak & Widget*
**Developer of record:** 동현 hwang (listed as "hbull") · **Bundle:** `com.hbull.daystamp`
**Corpus:** 779 written reviews · 29 storefronts · 30 March 2019 → 27 August 2026 (**7 years 5 months**)
**Corpus mean rating:** 4.134 · **Extracted:** 8 September 2026 (`itunes MZStore userReviewsRow`)
**Analysis date:** 12 September 2026
**Business model (review-derived):** Free download, **ad-supported**, with an **interstitial ad gate on habit creation**, a **free-tier habit cap**, and a paid **Premium** tier that reviewers describe first as a one-time lifetime purchase (₩9,900 → ₩29,000; ¥1,600 → ¥4,500; $13 → $30–35) and, from late 2025, as a **subscription** (§2.2).

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified finding gives **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal label, and review IDs**. Every claim traces to an exact record via `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`. **§9.6 is a complete 779-row index** — every review in the corpus, with ID, storefront, star rating, date, verbatim title and assigned themes. Nothing in this report is unreachable from that table.
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive** — one review can carry several. All 779 records carry at least one theme (mean 2.44 themes per review, max 10).
- Signal thresholds: `<0.1%` ignore · `0.1–0.5%` weak · `0.5–1%` emerging · `1–3%` meaningful · `3–5%` very strong · `>5%` high-priority. Denominator for every global percentage is **779**. **At n = 779, one review is 0.13%** — the "ignore" band is unreachable and a single review lands in "weak". Country-level percentages use that country's own denominator and say so.
- **Facts** are what reviewers wrote. **Interpretation** is labelled as such.
- Korean, Japanese, Chinese, German, Spanish, Portuguese, French and Hungarian reviews were read in the original. Translations are the analyst's and are marked with `/`; the original is always given first.

### ⚠️ Ten warnings before any number here is used

1. **This is a Korean app with a Korean corpus.** **605 of 779 reviews (77.66%) come from the Korean storefront**; the whole rest of the world is 174 (22.34%). Every global percentage in this report is dominated by Korea. Wherever a global number and the Korean number diverge, both are given. §6 splits KR from non-KR on every material theme, and several themes invert across that line (ads: 2.48% KR vs 9.20% non-KR; price: 0.99% KR vs 8.05% non-KR).
2. **Only two storefronts clear the 50-review bar**: Korea (605) and the United States (66). **Japan (26) does not**, and is reported throughout as limited evidence despite being the most analytically interesting small storefront. Every other storefront has ≤ 12 reviews and carries no standalone claim.
3. **The corpus spans 7.4 years and the product changed underneath it.** A 2019 review and a 2026 review are not describing the same app: widgets did not exist in 2019, Apple Watch support arrived in 2020, ads arrived around 2019–2020 and hardened into a habit-creation gate by 2021, the free-tier cap tightened repeatedly from 2023, and a subscription appears in 2025–2026 text. **No global percentage should be read as "what the app is like now"** — §7 is the only part of this report that answers that question.
4. **The rating decline is real, monotonic and recent.** Mean rating by year: 4.37 (2019) · 4.28 · 4.29 · 4.38 · 4.03 · 3.97 · 3.71 · **3.30 (2026, partial to 27 Aug)**. That is a 1.07-star fall from the 2022 peak. Sample sizes shrink alongside it (2026 n = 37), so the 2026 figure is the least stable number in the report.
5. **Two review bursts exist and both are defect bursts, not solicitation.** **21 of the 22 `bug_keyboard_memo` reviews land on 17–19 September 2020** (11 + 9 + 1) — a post-update keyboard failure. **Six of the 18 `bug_app_crash` reviews land on 15 June 2020.** No reviewer anywhere in the corpus mentions being asked for a review, and no rating-inflation pattern was found. **Interpretation: the bursts inflate 2020's defect share and nothing else. They are disclosed rather than removed, and the reviews in them are genuine.**
6. **Vote data is almost entirely empty and cannot be used.** 61 of 779 records (7.83%) have any `vote_count`; the maximum is **3**. No cluster can be detected from votes. **6 records (0.77%) are `is_edited`** — all Korean, and two of them visibly record an opinion changing (`7224428696`, `11680260937`).
7. **There is no version field in this schema.** Defects cannot be tied to a release except through what reviewers say. Where a reviewer names a version (`14358584372`: *"3.1.1로 업뎃했는데도 안돼요"* / "still broken after updating to 3.1.1") it is quoted as their claim.
8. **Classification was hand-curated, not automated.** All 779 records were read individually and in full, in their original language. 163 themes, every one a hand-assigned ID list. No regex or clustering assigned any theme; keyword sweeps were run *afterwards* only as an audit **against** the hand map, and the 19 genuine boundary errors they found were corrected and are enumerated at §9.1. The map is `temp/57-review-classification.py` and is validated programmatically (§1.5).
9. **"Paid" means the reviewer said so.** 99 reviewers (12.71%) state they purchased Premium. That is **not a conversion rate** and must never be read as one — it is the share of *written reviews* that mention paying. Reviewers who paid silently are invisible.
10. **Rating and text disagree often, in both directions.** **162 of the 450 five-star reviews (36.00% of that segment) carry at least one negative-direction theme** — in Korean reviewing convention a bug report is frequently filed at 5★. Conversely every one of the 72 one-star reviews carries a negative theme. **Star rating alone is a poor sentiment proxy in this corpus and is never used as one here.**

---

# EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

**1. The interstitial ad on habit creation is the single most destructive thing in this product, and it converts curiosity into deletion.**
**47 of 779 reviewers (6.03%, high-priority) complain about ads** — 31 generally (3.98%), **26 specifically that creating a habit requires watching an ad** (3.34%), and 8 that the ad gate itself is broken (1.03%). The ads family carries **mean ★ 2.234**, the lowest of any large family in the corpus, and **20 of the 25 reviewers who state they deleted the app (80%) name ads as the reason.** *"할일 하나 추가할때마다 광고...ㅋㅋㅋ ... 불편해서 결국 삭제합니다"* / "an ad every time I add a task lol … it's so annoying I'm deleting it" (`8509076402`, KR, 1★); *"A full screen ad after just creating one habit? Wow, immediately uninstalled"* (`8077608265`, CZ, 1★); *"The developer is losing a lot of app sales because the full screen ads are so horrible that you will stop using the app before finding out if you like the functionality"* (`12306162617`, US, 1★); *"Ad vomit … Want to create a group? First watch an ad. Want to add an item to your group? Another ad"* (`9783504520`, US, 1★). **Interpretation: the gate is placed at the exact moment of highest intent — setup — and it is the only mechanic in the corpus that reliably produces a 1★ and an uninstall in the same review.**

**2. The ad gate is also unreliable, which turns an annoyance into a dead end.**
**8 reviewers (1.03%, meaningful) report that the required ad fails to appear or fails to dismiss, leaving them unable to create a habit at all** — and **7 of the 8 are from 2024 onward**, so this is a live defect, not history. *"Can't add a task - no ads to show"* (`12589912745`, GB, 1★); *"광고 보고 추가하려는데 광고 안 떠서 추가를 아예 못해요"* / "I try to watch the ad to add one, but the ad doesn't load so I can't add anything at all" (`14465530297`, KR, 3★, 24 Aug 2026 — **the second-most-recent review in the corpus**); *"목표 세개 추가하는데 광고 7번 봐야됨요.. 일부러 이렇게 설정한건가;; 좋은 습관 만들려다가 승질나서 지워여"* / "seven ads to add three goals … is this deliberate? I got angry trying to build good habits and deleted it" (`12548739794`, KR); *"For some time I was trying to add another habit, but because of some lack of ads I cannot"* (`10862244835`, PL, 3★). **Interpretation: a monetization mechanic that can hard-block the product's core action is a reliability bug, not a pricing decision.**

**3. The widget is simultaneously the most-loved feature and the largest defect surface. Both are true and they are the same feature.**
**129 reviewers (16.56%, high-priority) raise a widget problem** — 90 (11.55%) a defect, 39 more a design limit. Against that, 30 (3.85%) praise the widget outright and several say it is why they installed. The defect breakdown: **50 (6.42%) the widget will not appear or goes blank**, 25 (3.21%) it does not reflect check-ins, 9 (1.16%) it reports a live group as "deleted", 6 (0.77%) it clips items. *"위젯 오류가 너무 많네요ㅠㅠ 제발 안정화 좀 해주세요ㅠㅠ 몇년째 쓰고있는데"* / "so many widget errors, please stabilise it, I've used this for years" (`12572214309`, KR, 1★); *"I absolutely love this app and I want to give it 5 stars, but the weekly widgets NEVER work"* (`13299429338`, US, 2★); *"업데이트 할때마다 위젯이 제대로 작동하지 않아서 ... 한번에 되지도 않아서 여러번 반복해야 합니다"* / "every single update breaks the widget and reinstalling doesn't even fix it the first time" (`11223902869`, KR, 1★). **Interpretation: widget reliability is the product's load-bearing wall. 23 of the 99 self-identified payers (23.2% of that segment) report a widget problem.**

**4. Paying does not stop the pain — and in the most recent era it is where the pain concentrates.**
**99 reviewers (12.71%) confirm they bought Premium. 31 of them (31.31% of payers) report a purchase-side failure**: 22 a post-purchase failure, 8 a failed restore, 3 Premium not applying, 2 a billing discrepancy, 1 a claim that did not match the listing. **That share rose from 4 of 31 payers in 2019–2021 to 21 of 46 payers in 2024–2026 (45.7%).** *"프리미엄 구매했는데, 습관이 5개밖에 안되요. 왜그러죠!!??"* / "I bought Premium and I still only get five habits. Why?!" (`12020319439`, KR, 1★); *"This app advertised a weekly visual habit widget as part of the lifetime premium purchase, but the feature does not work. I contacted the developer … they told me to wait … They also ignored my follow-up email"* (`14185972618`, CA, 1★); *"I bought this app because in the premium option they said that it was compatible to share with family and now that I have paid it is not compatible"* (`10724795076`, MX, 1★). **Interpretation: the corpus contains far more evidence of post-purchase disappointment than of pre-purchase objection. Refunds are rare (8, 1.03%) because most unhappy payers do not ask for money back — they write a 1★ and stay broken.**

**5. A concentrated, externally-corroborated Premium outage in July 2026 produced the worst week in the corpus.**
**Eight reviews between 24 and 28 July 2026** — six of them 1★ — all say the same thing: Premium was bought, and it does not work. `14340621141` (*"복구하기가 왜 되지 않나요?"* / "why won't restore work?"), `14340812621`, `14346556200` (*"구독햇는데 자꾸 오류가 떠서 위젯설정이 안되네여"* / "I subscribed but errors keep appearing and I can't configure the widget"), `14350226011`, `14351907761` (*"유료구매했는데 위젯설정안됨 … 이럴거면 유료구매왜함"* / "paid and the widget still won't configure … why did I even pay"), `14351975658` (*"프리미엄 사용중이었고 안되서 다시 프리미엄 연간으로 재구매했는데 안되네요 환불 부탁드려요"* / "I was on Premium, it stopped working, I re-bought the annual Premium and it still doesn't work — refund please"), `14352377997` (HK, *"I paid for the premium and now it asks for subscription??! DO NOT BUY IT"*), `14358584372` (*"3.1.1로 업뎃했는데도 안돼요"*). **Publicly reported release notes independently confirm a Premium-activation defect in version 3.1.0 that was fixed in 3.1.1** (§2.3, §9.5). **Interpretation: this is the clearest cause-and-effect chain in the dataset — one broken release, eight furious paying customers, in five days, at the very end of the corpus.**

**6. Cross-device is the largest unmet need in the product's history and it has never been resolved.**
**72 reviewers (9.24%, high-priority) ask for sync, an iPad build, a Mac build, or a usable backup** — 44 (5.65%) specifically iCloud/device sync, 21 (2.70%) an iPad version, 8 (1.03%) a Mac version, 17 (2.18%) backup/export. The demand is **flat across all three eras** (24 / 23 / 25 reviews) — it neither grew nor was answered. *"이 앱은 정말 완벽합니다"* is how they usually open, then: *"동기화 시켜주십쇼,, 저 같은 경우는 아이패드, 아이폰 다 같이 쓰는데 ... 자꾸 백업해야 하는 일이 생깁니다"* / "please give us sync — I use iPad and iPhone and keep having to back up manually" (`8942549944`, KR, 5★); *"No Syncing Across Devices … being restricted to one device makes this app useless to me"* (`10513784132`, US, 1★); *"아이패드와 맥북 호환여부를 오래전부터 질문 한 분들이 많은데 매번 예정이라고 하던데 진짜 되긴 하나요?"* / "people have been asking about iPad and Mac for years and you always say it's planned — is it ever actually happening?" (`11361971544`, KR, 4★). **One Japanese reviewer reports it working** — *"icloud連携ができるようになって、iPhoneとipadで同じデータが共有できるようになりました！"* / "iCloud linking works now and my iPhone and iPad share the same data!" (`11710806302`, JP, Sep 2024) — **yet six Korean reviewers after that date still say it does not work for them.** **Interpretation: either the capability is Premium-gated and undiscoverable, or it is partial. Both readings are supported and both are a problem.**

**7. What people love is restraint, and they say so in the same words for seven straight years.**
**198 reviewers (25.42%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.753) praise the UX**: simplicity/minimalism 95 (12.20%), ease of use 81 (10.40%), visual design 69 (8.86%), colour customisation 22 (2.82%). The vocabulary barely changes between 2019 and 2026 — *깔끔* (clean), *심플*, *직관적* (intuitive), *군더더기 없는* (no fat). *"다른 앱들은 뭔가 기능이 너무 많고 시각화에 너무 집중해서 좀 그랬는데, 이 앱은 정말 딱 제가 원하는 기능만 있어서 ... 복잡하게 뭐 더 안넣고 지금의 간결함을 최대한 유지했으면 좋겠습니다"* / "other apps have too many features … this one has exactly what I want. Please don't add complexity — keep this leanness" (`6416619753`, KR, 5★); *"It's extremely simple to use and does exactly what I want. Other apps tried to do way too much"* (`8241797483`, US, 5★). **Interpretation: every feature request in §3.4 must be weighed against this. The most-praised property of the product is the absence of features.**

**8. Displacement evidence is strong: 69 reviewers (8.86%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.884) chose this app after trying the category.**
Not generic praise — these reviewers name the search. *"해빗트래커 유목민이라 10가지 넘게 써보고 정착했습니다"* / "I'm a habit-tracker nomad, tried over ten and settled here" (`9520902510`, KR); *"I've tried 10 habit tracking apps at this point, including most of the ones with 1k reviews. I think this one actually wins in for the insightful data"* (`6522788831`, US); *"수많은 목표관리 앱을 직접 사용하고 비교한 결과 이 어플이 최고임"* / "after personally using and comparing countless goal apps, this one is the best" (`7119448731`, KR); *"존재하는 온갖 습관 관리 앱 다 써봤는데 이게 제일 깔끔해서 결국 돌아오게 됩니다"* / "I've tried every habit app there is and keep coming back because this is the cleanest" (`6851822317`, KR). **But it is decaying: 10.36% of E1 reviews, 11.11% of E2, and only 4.23% of E3 (2024–2026).**

**9. Korean users do not write reviews — they write support tickets in the review box.**
**50 reviewers (6.42%, high-priority) address a direct question to the developer inside their review**, and **49 of the 50 are Korean (8.10% of KR reviews vs 0.57% of non-KR)**. Most are 5★ and most concern things the app can already do: how to delete a habit, where the widget settings are, whether iCloud works, what the archive is. Alongside them, **27 reviewers (3.47%) cannot find how to delete or edit a habit** and **23 (2.95%) say there is no guidance at all**. One Korean reviewer wrote a public how-to for everyone else: *"열분! 별점 한 개 중에 수정이나 삭제 어케하냐고 물어보시등가 ... 알려드릴께엽"* / "Everyone! For all the 1★ reviewers asking how to edit or delete — let me explain" (`7781598096`, KR, 5★). **Interpretation: an in-app help surface and a real support channel would remove a measurable share of the negative reviews. Six reviewers say they could not find how to contact the developer at all; eight say the developer never replied.**

**10. The monetization model changed at least three times and every change is visible as anger in the corpus.**
**10 reviewers (1.28%, meaningful, mean ★ 1.700 — the lowest mean of any theme with n ≥ 5) complain that the terms changed under them.** The price points they quote, in date order: ₩9,900 (Dec 2020, `6810145127`) → ¥1,600 (May 2022, `8685490868`) → $13 (Oct 2022, `9239739610`) → ₩29,000 (Jan 2024, `10807761181`) → ¥4,500 (Jan 2024, `10860902855`, who explicitly notes the rise from ¥1,600) → $30 (Mar 2025, `12446921299`) → $35 (Nov 2023, `10562732428`, UA). Then the terms themselves: *"At first unlimited habits were free but now only three are for free?"* (`12795320709`, PK); *"Changed the data retention policy to 30 days for the free version"* (`13592161917`, US, 1★); *"急にデイリーチェックインが有料機能になりましたか？... おしらせにもないし"* / "did daily check-in suddenly become a paid feature? There was no announcement" (`13169190954`, JP, 2★); *"Have been using this app for years and suddenly it's no longer possible to increase the interval for a daily goal (e. 3x a day). What's going on? :("* (`13386826119`, SG, 2★); *"I paid for the premium and now it asks for subscription??!"* (`14352377997`, HK, 1★). **Interpretation: each tightening was applied silently to existing users. The corpus contains no example of a change being announced, and several reviewers explicitly say they only discovered it when something they relied on stopped working.**

**11. Price resistance is real but it is almost entirely outside Korea.**
**20 reviewers (2.57%) say Premium is too expensive — 14 of them non-Korean (8.05% of the 174 non-KR reviews) against 6 Korean (0.99% of 605).** Japan is the epicentre: **7 of 26 Japanese reviews (26.92% of JP — limited evidence, n < 50) object to the price**, two of them specifically to the ¥1,600 → ¥4,500 increase. *"プレミアムに登録しないと色々な制限があり、金額も4500円とかなり高額なので諦めました……。他のレビューで1600円と書かれている方がいますが、かなり値上げしたのですね"* / "Premium has all these restrictions and ¥4,500 is steep, so I gave up. Another review mentions ¥1,600 — so it went up a lot" (`10860902855`, JP, 5★); *"I would love to use Premium features. But 35$ for habit tracker is way too much"* (`10562732428`, UA); *"买断费用稍贵，能减少？"* / "the buyout price is a bit expensive, could it come down?" (`5627754008`, CN). **Three reviewers propose the fix themselves — unbundle it** (`7028234235` CA, `11510979900` JP, `10639075443` PL), and one asks outright for a monthly plan instead of a lifetime one.

**12. The cheapest high-value moves, in evidence order:** fix the Premium-activation and restore path and publish what happened in July 2026 (finding 5; 8 reviews in 5 days, externally corroborated) → **move the interstitial off habit creation** (findings 1–2; 47 reviews, mean ★ 2.23, 80% of all stated uninstalls) → stabilise the widget, starting with "widget will not appear" and the phantom "group deleted" (finding 3; 90 defect reviews, 23% of payers) → ship or clearly document cross-device sync and an iPad build (finding 6; 72 reviews, flat for 7 years) → make delete/edit discoverable and add an in-app help and support link (finding 9; 50 + 27 + 23 reviews, nearly all Korean, nearly all 5★ and therefore free to fix) → announce monetization changes before they land and grandfather existing behaviour (finding 10; mean ★ 1.700) → unbundle Premium or offer a trial for the non-Korean markets that are price-resistant (finding 11) → do **not** add features to the main surface (finding 7).

---

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `reviews.jsonl` | Primary corpus — 779 records, one JSON object per line |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 29 per-storefront files — reconciled exactly to the main file |
| `manifest.json` | App metadata, per-storefront counts, rating distribution, mean |
| `_state.json` | Extraction state. **69 storefronts were queried; all 69 report `complete: true`; 29 returned reviews and 40 returned zero.** Used to confirm that empty storefronts were actually queried. |
| Publicly reported App Store release notes for this app ID (external) | Accessed 12 September 2026 via search-engine summary. **Low–medium confidence** — the App Store page itself is egress-blocked from this environment. Used only in §2.3 and §5.5, always labelled. |
| Publicly reported App Store consumer-spend market rankings (external) | Accessed 12 September 2026 — used only to define the high-spend group in §6.6, always labelled |

## 1.2 Schema

Fields present on every one of the 779 records: `review_id`, `app_id`, `app_name`, `country`, `country_name`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `author`, `date`, `vote_count`, `vote_sum`, `is_edited`.

**Fields used in this analysis:** `review_id`, `country`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `date`.
**Fields inspected and set aside:** `author` (not analysed; no reviewer is named in this report), `app_id` / `app_name` / `country_name` (constant or derivable from `country`).
**Fields inspected and used only as integrity checks:**
- `vote_count` / `vote_sum` — non-zero on **61 / 49** records respectively (7.83% / 6.29%). **The maximum `vote_count` in the whole corpus is 3.** Vote data is too sparse to detect inflated clusters; the burst checks in warning 5 rely on date distribution alone.
- `is_edited` — true on **6 records (0.77%)**, all Korean: `5875043124` (2020-04-28, 5★), `8451895584` (2022-03-13, 2★), `11680260937` (2024-09-02, 5★), `11890852416` (2024-10-30, 5★), `12578730431` (2025-04-24, 5★), `14354171704` (2026-07-27, 5★). Two more reviewers edited in place without the flag being set and say so in the text (`7224428696`: *"(수정) 사용자화 다시 생겨서 너무 좋아욤!!!"* / "(edit) the custom widget is back, I love it"; `12135641738`: *"(수정) 해결 됨!"* / "(edit) solved!").

**Text handling:** HTML entities (`&#39;`, `&#34;`, `&amp;`) are present in the raw `title` and `body` fields and were unescaped before reading and classification. Body length: median **71** characters, mean 108.7, min **1** (`9409544493`, KR — body is the single syllable *"굿"* / "good"), max **1941** (`13574542005`, RU — a two-part structured feature proposal). Body length by rating: 5★ median 75 · 4★ 69 · 3★ 68 · 2★ 82 · 1★ 61. **Short reviews are not concentrated in any rating band.**

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Lines in `reviews.jsonl` | **779** |
| Records parsed successfully | **779 (100%)** |
| Unique `review_id` | **779 — zero duplicate IDs** |
| Records with empty `body` | 0 |
| Records with empty `title` | 0 |
| Records missing any schema field | 0 |
| Records summed across `by_country/*.jsonl` | **779**, all unique |
| IDs in country files not present in main file | **0** |
| IDs in main file not present in any country file | **0** |
| Records where `country` field ≠ country filename | **0** |
| **Records read individually and classified** | **779 (100%)** |
| Records carrying ≥1 assigned theme | **779 (100%)** |
| Denominator used for every global percentage | **779** |

`manifest.json` states 779 total reviews, 29 storefronts with reviews, mean 4.134 and a rating distribution of 5:450 / 4:156 / 3:72 / 2:29 / 1:72. **Independent recomputation from the raw records reproduces all of these exactly** (recomputed mean 4.13350). Per-storefront counts in `manifest.json` also reproduce exactly against the merged file.

**Deduplication: none performed, and none was needed.** Zero exact title+body duplicate pairs exist in this corpus.

**Zero-review storefronts:** `_state.json` records `complete: true, collected: 0` for **40 storefronts**, including `fr`, `nl`, `se`, `dk`, `no`, `fi`, `ch`, `tr`, `id`, `th`, `ae`, `eg`, `il`, `cl`, `co`, `ar`. These were queried and returned no written reviews. **Their absence is a real observation about this app's distribution, not a gap in extraction** — the app has essentially no written-review footprint in continental Europe outside DE/ES/PL/CZ/HU/IT/AT, or in South-East Asia outside SG/MY/VN/PH.

## 1.4 Date range and shape

- First review: **30 March 2019** — two records share this date: `3948640864` (KR, 5★, *"매우 깔끔"* / "very clean") and `3943711457` (US, 5★, *"Love it"*).
- Last review: **27 August 2026** (`14476535510`, KR, 5★ — a widget background-refresh question).
- Span: **2,708 days (7 years 5 months).** 779 reviews across **639 distinct days**.

| Year | n | % of corpus | Mean ★ | 5★ | 4★ | 3★ | 2★ | 1★ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2019 (from 30 Mar) | 68 | 8.73% | 4.368 | 43 | 15 | 6 | 0 | 4 |
| 2020 | 179 | 22.98% | 4.285 | 99 | 51 | 17 | 5 | 7 |
| 2021 | 139 | 17.84% | 4.288 | 90 | 22 | 13 | 5 | 9 |
| 2022 | 92 | 11.81% | **4.380** | 62 | 16 | 6 | 3 | 5 |
| 2023 | 88 | 11.30% | 4.034 | 48 | 19 | 8 | 2 | 11 |
| 2024 | 104 | 13.35% | 3.971 | 56 | 20 | 12 | 1 | 15 |
| 2025 | 72 | 9.24% | 3.708 | 38 | 7 | 5 | 12 | 10 |
| 2026 (to 27 Aug) | 37 | 4.75% | **3.297** | 14 | 6 | 5 | 1 | 11 |

**Bursts.** Maximum reviews on a single day is **12 (2020-09-17)**, followed by 9 (2020-09-18) and 6 (2020-06-15). All three are defect bursts:
- **2020-09-17/18/19: 21 of the corpus's 22 `bug_keyboard_memo` reviews** — the note keyboard stopped appearing after an update. Ratings in that cluster: 10×5★, 7×4★, 2×3★, 3×2★ — **the users were furious and still gave 5 stars**, which is warning 10 in miniature.
- **2020-06-14/15/17: 8 of the 18 `bug_app_crash` reviews** — the app would not launch after an update.
- **2026-07-24 to 2026-07-28: the Premium-activation cluster** (§5.5) — 8 reviews across 5 days, not a single-day spike but the densest negative cluster in the corpus's final year.

**Interpretation of the shape:** volume peaks in 2020 (179) and declines steadily thereafter while the mean rating falls. **A shrinking, angrier review stream is the corpus's overall trajectory**, and §7 tests which themes drive it.

## 1.5 Processing method

1. **Parse and reconcile.** All 779 records parsed from `reviews.jsonl`; reconciled against `by_country/*.jsonl`, `manifest.json` and `_state.json` (§1.3). Script: `temp/57-reconcile.py`.
2. **Full-text dump.** Every record written out in full, HTML-unescaped, sorted by storefront then date, with ID, country, rating, date, vote counts and edit flag. Script: `temp/57-dump.py` → `temp/57-all-reviews.txt` (779 records, 224 KB).
3. **Individual reading.** All 779 reviews read one by one, in original language. No sampling, no keyword filtering, no truncation, no stopping at a context limit. Languages read in the original: **Korean (605), English, Japanese (26), Chinese (4), German (3), Spanish (4), Portuguese (3), French (1), Hungarian (1)**.
4. **Hand-curated classification.** A theme → review-ID map built by hand, **163 themes**, saved at `temp/57-review-classification.py`. No regex or automated clustering was used to assign any theme. The map is keyed by each record's **ordinal position in the canonical sort**, and the validator resolves ordinals back to `review_id`, so **no review ID was transcribed by hand anywhere in this analysis**.
5. **Programmatic validation** (`temp/57-validate.py`), run to completion before any number in this report was written:

| Validation check | Result |
|---|---|
| Review IDs in the map that do not exist in `reviews.jsonl` | **0 ✓** |
| Duplicate IDs within any single theme | **0 ✓** |
| Records in the corpus with no theme assigned | **0 ✓** |
| Themes with an empty ID list | **0 ✓** |
| Themes declared in metadata but never used | **0 ✓** |
| Records carrying ≥1 theme | **779 / 779 ✓** |
| Themes per review | min 1 · max 10 · mean 2.44 |

6. **Keyword audit of the hand map.** After classification, twelve keyword sweeps (ads, purchase, widget, sync, price, refund, watch, iPad, Android, data loss, delete, crash) plus a praise-vocabulary sweep were run *against* the hand map to find disagreements — not to build it. **19 genuine boundary errors were found and corrected**; every other hit was a regex false positive. Both are enumerated at §9.1.
7. **Aggregation.** All counts, percentages, means, rating splits, country cuts, year/era buckets, family unions and cross-tabs computed from the validated map (`temp/57-stats.py`, `temp/57-stats2.py`, `temp/57-stats3.py`). Appendix tables §9.2, §9.3 and §9.6 are machine-generated from the same map (`temp/57-appendix.py`).

**Known error risk.** The judgement calls with the widest error bars are: (a) `simplicity_minimal` vs `ease_of_use` — Korean *깔끔* and *직관적* overlap heavily, so the two are reported separately **and** as a union with `aesthetic_design` (§3.2), and no finding depends on the split; (b) `bug_widget_not_showing` vs `bug_widget_not_updating` — "the widget is blank" and "the widget doesn't reflect my check-in" are sometimes the same underlying fault described differently, so §3.3.2 reports the widget-defect union first and the split second; (c) `paid_premium_confirmed` requires the reviewer to state they bought it, which **excludes the 6 `accidental_purchase` reviewers** who paid by mistake and wanted out — a deliberate boundary, documented at §9.1; (d) `question_to_developer` is assigned only when the reviewer directs a question at the developer, not when they merely express confusion.

## 1.6 Limitations and known biases

- **Written reviews are not users.** 779 written reviews over 7.4 years are a small, self-selected slice. Nothing here estimates install base, retention or conversion.
- **Korea dominates (77.66%).** Warning 1. Every global figure is a Korean figure with a 22% international remainder attached. §6 is the corrective.
- **Only KR and US clear n = 50.** Warning 2. Japan, the most price-sensitive storefront in the corpus, is limited evidence at n = 26 and is labelled as such every time it appears.
- **The product changed across the window.** Warning 3. Themes that are absent early (widgets, ads, subscription) are absent because the feature did not exist, not because nobody cared.
- **Two defect bursts inflate 2020.** Warning 5. The 2020 defect share should be read with the 21-review keyboard cluster in mind.
- **No version field.** Warning 7. Only two reviewers name a version at all.
- **Vote and edit data are too sparse to use.** Warning 6.
- **Star rating is a weak sentiment proxy here.** Warning 10 — 36.00% of five-star reviews carry a negative theme.
- **External verification is summary-only.** The App Store listing and `itunes.apple.com/lookup` are both egress-blocked from this environment; §2.3 corroborates but does not confirm, and **no finding in Parts 3–8 rests on an external source**.
- **At n = 779, one review is 0.13%.** 29 themes have n = 1 and every one of them is labelled "weak" and enumerated by ID. They are recorded, not promoted.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 Feature inventory derived from reviews

Every capability below is named by at least one reviewer. Nothing here is inferred from the store listing.

| Capability | First evidenced | Review evidence (IDs) | Notes from the corpus |
|---|---|---|---|
| Habit / activity list with a "stamp" check-in | 2019-03-30 | `3948640864`, `3960542933`, `3968135443` | The core metaphor: *도장* (a seal/stamp). 19 reviewers praise the act itself. |
| Per-habit colour selection | 2019-09-05 | `4731599171`, `5419808409`, `6861085361`, `11676376793` | 22 reviewers praise it; 4 want more colours |
| Per-check-in notes / memo | 2019-04-02 | `3968135443`, `4519791548`, `6522788831` | 21 reviewers praise; 9 want notes on *missed* days too |
| Calendar view with completion dots | 2019-08-09 | `4591645326`, `6437355632`, `6061506881` | 11 praise; 4 want line/continuous rendering |
| Weekly / monthly / yearly statistics and reports | 2019-03-31 | `3952930062`, `7925046804`, `13064021764` | 24 praise. `13064021764` calls the yearly report unique in the category |
| Reminders / notifications, per weekday | 2019-09-06 | `4734681372`, `6600719221`, `13045863870` | 10 praise; 5 want multiple per day |
| Groups / categories | 2020-06-04 | `6034586841`, `7273860928`, `9560536416` | 7 praise. **Premium-gated in Japan's reading** (`8685490868`) |
| Home-screen widgets (3 / 6 / 14 slots, small / medium / large) | 2020-10-05 | `6502762176`, `10887026952`, `10356701753` | 30 praise; 129 raise a problem |
| Apple Watch app | 2020-03-06 | `5621508960`, `6359087808`, `9537581546` | 8 praise; 26 report it hanging on "Loading…" |
| Streak counter | 2020-02-11 | `5516233972` (thanks the dev for shipping it), `5360484203` (requested it) | Shipped between Jan and Feb 2020 |
| Dropbox backup / restore | 2020-01-03 | `5353897655` (*"백업기능이 생겨서 너무 좋습니다"* / "so glad backup arrived"), `6160193157`, `11338564382` | Manual, not automatic; 17 want more |
| iCloud sync | 2024-09-10 | `11710806302` (JP) — the **only** review reporting it working | 44 reviewers still ask for it, several after that date |
| Daily check-in count / "goal" (n times per day) | 2023-01-15 | `9511684630`, `12095285181`, `10887501928` | Capped at 5/day (`11315669095`, `10977856960`); **later restricted by tier** (`13169190954`, `13386826119`, `12570234049`) |
| Archive ("보관함") for finished habits | 2022-02-08 | `8332637759`, `13064021764` | Reviewers report losing the record when archiving |
| Skip / rest-day marking | 2024-06-22 | `11410069153` (*"Thank you for your continuous effort… I'm really pleased that I can use this feature, which I've been eagerly waiting for"*) | Shipped ~mid-2024; still only reachable from the calendar step |
| Font selection | 2024-01-27 | `10869361826` (JP), `13034127559` (KR) | 2 praise |
| Photo attachment | 2024-03-04 | `11005825245` (*"프리미엄을 구매해도 사진등록은 3개밖에 못하나요?"* / "even with Premium can I only attach 3 photos?") | Exists but capped; 9 reviewers request/expand it |
| Shortcuts integration ("Check-in ID") | 2025-07-28 | `12946620398` (JP) — exists but undocumented | |
| AI habit suggestion | 2025-08-05 | `12978533903`, `13130410310` — **both say they cannot find it** | Shipped 2025; zero reviewers report using it successfully |
| In-app suggestion / bug-report form | 2026-04-16 | `13962223534` (JP) — **crashes on Submit** | |
| Family Sharing | 2023-12-21 | `10724795076` (MX) — **advertised, did not work for them** | |

**Capabilities reviewers ask for that no reviewer reports existing:** Android build (13), Mac build (8), iPad-native layout (21), per-weekday display filtering that actually filters (16 requests + 8 defect reports), memo on a missed day (9), history of past weekly/monthly reports (11), unlimited/uncapped daily check-ins (24), habit-level notes (3), full-text search (2), social/friends (2), quit-habit (inverse) tracking (1), Apple Health integration (1).

## 2.2 Free / paid / trial classification, reconstructed from review text

| Capability | Status per the corpus | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Download and core tracking | **Free** | `9026380199` (*"Simple, easy, straightforward, no fuss. Free"*), `11031107771` (*"Easy to use and it's free"*), `5604731898` (*"이게 공짜라니"* / "I can't believe this is free") |
| Creating a habit beyond the free cap | **Ad-gated, then paywalled** | `10476012561` (*"투두 세개만들면 광고가 떠요"* / "make three to-dos and an ad appears"), `12474152923` (*"3-4개인가 넘으면 목표 추가 하려고 시도할때마다 광고봐야함"*), `12446921299` (US, *"it won't let me without paying $30"*), `12795320709` (PK, *"At first unlimited habits were free but now only three are for free?"*) |
| Free-tier habit ceiling | **Tightened over time — 20 reviewers (2.57%)** | 2020–2021: 3 reviews · 2023: 1 · **2024–2026: 16.** `12020319439` reports being capped at 5 *after* paying |
| Banner + interstitial ads | **Free tier only; removed by Premium** | `8235594886` (*"광고제거때문에 프리미엄 결제하고싶은데"* / "I want to buy Premium just to remove ads"), `8751577977` (*"Paying for Premium is a definite must have - ads are extremely bothersome without it"*) |
| Dark mode | **Premium (US reading)** | `9239739610` (*"I have to shell out $13 for premium just for dark mode"*) |
| Groups | **Premium (JP reading)** | `8685490868` (*"グループ分けはプレミアムのみの機能でした"* / "grouping turned out to be Premium-only") |
| Backup / restore | **Premium** | `11103760055` (*"구매를 안 하면 아예 백업 안 된다~ 이러고 있네요^^.. 참나.. 협박도 아니고"* / "so you can't back up at all unless you buy — this is practically blackmail"), `11222810326`, `10869361826` (JP) |
| Data retention beyond 30 days | **Premium, from ~2026** | `13592161917` (US, 1★, *"Changed the data retention policy to 30 days for the free version"*), `6181430289` (2020, wanted to buy precisely *"전체기록 보유하고 싶으니"* / "because I want to keep the full record") |
| Multiple check-ins per day (>1) | **Became Premium-gated** | `13169190954` (JP, *"急にデイリーチェックインが有料機能になりましたか？"*), `13386826119` (SG), `12570234049` (KR, describes previously-free items being retroactively capped to one) |
| Calendar / weekly widget | **Premium** | `9488882585` (*"프리미엄 달력위젯"*), `10747044142` (*"프리미엄 전용 위젯"*), `14185972618` (CA, bought lifetime **for** the weekly widget) |
| Premium pricing model | **One-time "lifetime" → later a subscription** | Lifetime: `7106407718` (*"Love the idea of lifetime subscription"*), `8419383942` (*"Just bought the lifetime premium"*), `6636232220` (ES, *"el pago es único sin ningún tipo de suscripción"* / "a single payment with no subscription at all"), `9541183862` (*"바로 영구결제했어요"*). Subscription: `14346556200` (*"구독햇는데"* / "I subscribed"), `14351975658` (*"프리미엄 연간으로 재구매"* / "re-bought Premium annual"), `14352377997` (HK, *"I paid for the premium and now it asks for subscription??!"*), `9224244598` (2022, *"구독취소 어떻게 하나요?"* / "how do I cancel the subscription?") |
| Free trial | **Does not exist — 3 reviewers ask for one** | `8301510290` (*"기간체험으로 만들어도 괜찮을 것 같습니다"* / "a time-limited trial would be good"), `10850332771` (*"무료 체험 2주 후 할인된 가격에 결제 이런거라든가"* / "how about a 2-week trial then a discounted price"), `12572214309` (*"무료 사용해보고 마음이 생겨야 유료로 바꿀 마음이 생기잖아요"* / "you only want to pay after trying it free") |

**Observed price points, in date order** (each is what that reviewer says they saw):

| Date | Storefront | Price quoted | Review |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2019-08-19 | KR | ₩3,994 (charged after a redeem code) | `4640684119` |
| 2020-12-30 | KR | ₩9,900 lifetime | `6810145127` |
| 2021-10-28 | KR | *"3만원 주고라도 구입했을 정도"* / "I'd have paid ₩30,000" — willingness, not price | `7961745633` |
| 2022-05-19 | JP | ¥1,600 (would pay ¥300) | `8685490868` |
| 2022-10-31 | US | $13 | `9239739610` |
| 2023-11-08 | UA | $35 | `10562732428` |
| 2024-01-11 | KR | ₩29,000 | `10807761181` |
| 2024-01-22 | KR | *"거의 3만원"* / "nearly ₩30,000"; would pay under ₩20,000 | `10850332771` |
| 2024-01-25 | JP | ¥4,500 (notes it was ¥1,600) | `10860902855` |
| 2024-02-20 | KR | listed ₩29,000, **card charged ₩44,000** | `10959845967` |
| 2024-07-19 | JP | ¥4,500; proposes a ¥2,000 tier | `11510979900` |
| 2025-03-21 | US | $30 | `12446921299` |

**Interpretation (labelled as interpretation):** the corpus is consistent with roughly a **3× lifetime price increase between 2020–2022 and 2024**, followed by a shift to subscription in 2025–2026. Two reviewers state the increase explicitly (`10860902855`, `11879711185`, both JP). **The corpus cannot confirm the current price or the current model** — it can only show what reviewers saw on the dates they wrote.

## 2.3 External sources — and where they agree with the corpus

`itunes.apple.com/lookup` and `apps.apple.com` are **egress-blocked** from this environment (HTTP 403 at the proxy on every attempt), so no primary store metadata — price, IAP list, version history, release date, rating count — could be read. Only search-engine summaries of the listing were obtainable. They are **low–medium confidence** and are used for corroboration only.

| External statement | Corpus agreement |
|---|---|
| Developer "hbull"; app is free to download | Matches `manifest.json` (`com.hbull.daystamp`) and the free-download evidence in §2.2 |
| **"In version 3.1.0 Premium may not be activated correctly; fixed in 3.1.1. Version 3.1.1 includes a fix for an issue related to applying Premium and restoring purchases."** | **Directly corroborates the July 2026 cluster** (§5.5): 8 reviews in 5 days about Premium not applying and restore failing, one of which names version 3.1.1 (`14358584372`). **This is the strongest external–internal match in the report.** |
| Listing describes "Simple and Powerful Widget Features … various widgets … from your iOS home screen" as a headline capability | Matches the corpus: the widget is both the top-praised named feature (30) and the top defect surface (129) |
| Listing states "some in-app purchases **and subscriptions** may be shareable with your family group when Family Sharing is enabled" | Corroborates **both** the subscription's existence (§2.2) and the specific complaint in `10724795076` (MX) that Family Sharing did not work as advertised |

**No finding in Parts 3–8 rests on an external source.** Every price, gate and capability statement in Part 2 is sourced to a named review ID.

# PART 3 — GLOBAL FINDINGS

All percentages below use the **global denominator 779** and the period **30 March 2019 – 27 August 2026** unless narrowed. Theme counts are non-exclusive. Complete ID lists for all 163 themes are at §9.3; the full 163-row quantified table is at §9.2.

## 3.1 Theme-family aggregates (deduplicated to unique reviewers)

| Family | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | What it contains |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Any request / unmet need** | **267** | 34.27% | high-priority | 4.449 | 57 `req_*` themes + widget-group selection |
| **Any reliability defect** | **229** | 29.40% | high-priority | 3.690 | 23 defect themes incl. `update_regression` |
| **UX praise (any)** | **198** | 25.42% | high-priority | 4.753 | simplicity ∪ ease ∪ design ∪ colour ∪ font |
| **Named-feature praise (any)** | **135** | 17.33% | high-priority | 4.785 | the 16 feature-praise themes |
| **Widget trouble (defect or design)** | **129** | 16.56% | high-priority | 3.992 | 4 widget defects + 4 widget design limits |
| **Widget defects only** | **90** | 11.55% | high-priority | 3.689 | not-showing ∪ not-updating ∪ group-deleted ∪ layout |
| **Monetization friction (any)** | **89** | 11.42% | high-priority | 2.989 | 19 themes: price, cap, paywall, refund, purchase failure |
| **Cross-device / platform gap** | **84** | 10.78% | high-priority | 4.405 | iCloud ∪ iPad ∪ Mac ∪ Android ∪ backup |
| **Usability friction (any)** | **71** | 9.11% | high-priority | 3.859 | delete/edit ∪ onboarding ∪ past-date ∪ check-in ∪ localization ∪ accessibility ∪ generic |
| **Ads (any)** | **47** | 6.03% | high-priority | **2.234** | complaint ∪ creation-gate ∪ broken |
| **Purchase intent (any)** | **36** | 4.62% | very strong | 4.361 | stated intent ∪ purchase question |
| **Paid-user trouble** | **36** | 4.62% | very strong | **2.528** | post-purchase ∪ restore ∪ not-applied ∪ billing ∪ purchase-failure ∪ misleading claim |
| **Churn (stated or risked)** | **27** | 3.47% | very strong | **1.593** | 25 stated deletions + 2 stated switching risk |
| **Monetization positive (any)** | **25** | 3.21% | very strong | 4.800 | free praise ∪ lifetime praise ∪ price-fair ∪ no-ads |
| **Support failure (any)** | **15** | 1.93% | meaningful | 2.333 | unresponsive ∪ channel unclear ∪ dismissive |

**Read this table as the shape of the whole corpus.** A third of reviewers want something added. Nearly a third hit a defect. A quarter praise the UX in the same three words. And the three lowest-rated families in the product — churn (1.593), ads (2.234) and paid-user trouble (2.528) — are all **monetization mechanics**, not features.
