## 3.3 Negative themes in detail

### 3.3.1 Ads — 47 reviews (6.03%, high-priority, mean ★ 2.234), the corpus's churn engine

**The single most consequential negative finding in this report.** Three sub-themes, non-exclusive:

| Sub-theme | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Window |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ads_complaint` — ads are too many / too intrusive | 31 | 3.98% | very strong | 1.903 | 2019-10-16 → 2026-06-29 |
| `ads_gate_habit_creation` — you must watch an ad to create a habit | 26 | 3.34% | very strong | **2.154** | 2021-01-26 → 2026-08-24 |
| `ads_broken` — the required ad fails, blocking creation entirely | 8 | 1.03% | meaningful | 2.875 | 2020-03-11 → 2026-08-24 |
| `ads_tolerable` — ads exist but the reviewer accepts them | 6 | 0.77% | emerging | **5.000** | 2021-03-04 → 2025-01-24 |
| **Union (complaint ∪ gate ∪ broken)** | **47** | **6.03%** | high-priority | **2.234** | 2019-10-16 → 2026-08-24 |

**Rating distribution of the ads union:** 1★ 23 · 2★ 7 · 3★ 8 · 4★ 1 · 5★ 8. **Half of every ads review is a one-star.**

**The churn link is the finding.** Of the **25 reviewers who state they deleted or are deleting the app**, **20 (80%) name ads**; 14 cite the volume, 13 the creation gate, 2 the broken ad. No other cause comes close — the next-largest is "can't find delete" at 2, and **zero** stated deletions cite a crash or data loss. *"광고가 생기면 앱에 머무르는 시간이 길어져서 효율성이 사라짐... 삭제함"* / "ads make you spend longer in the app so the efficiency disappears — deleted" (`5672929597`, KR, 1★); *"몇년째쓰다가 삭제합니다"* / "used it for years, deleting it now" (`10346163428`, KR, 1★ — title: *"와..어떻게 계획생성시 광고넣을생각을"* / "wow, whose idea was it to put an ad on plan creation"); *"Impossible to use because of the millions of ads. Would love this app otherwise"* (`10269881878`, US, 1★); *"I downloaded this to help myself manage my adhd struggles but as soon as I made a single activity scheduled it gave me an add. Literally distracted me before I could get familiarized with the app. Opposite of helpful for me"* (`6914248951`, US, 2★).

**The ad length escalated.** Reviewers quote **5 seconds** in 2021 (`7877990468`, `8110196858`), **29–30 seconds** from 2023 (`10614381628`, `10353308106`, `11543927654`, `14240219624`). `10353308106` (KR, 1★, Sep 2023) ties the two halves of the monetization together in one sentence: *"평생구매 가격이 생각보다 너무 비싸길래 무료로 이용하고 있었는데, 이제는 습관 만드는 것 까지 광고 30초를 붙이셨네요ㅠㅠ 점점 광고가 심해져서 그냥 탈주하려구요"* / "the lifetime price was more than I expected so I stayed free — now you've put a 30-second ad on even creating a habit. The ads keep getting worse so I'm just leaving."

**Six reviewers accept the ads, and every one of them gives 5★** (`7063081300` IN, `7108755746` US, `10263515783` KR, `11042165108` US, `11510979900` JP, `12224924926` CA). What they have in common is that they describe the ad as **short and confined to creation**: *"중간에 광고가 있지만 짧아서 불편함을 느끼지는 않았어요"* / "there are ads but they're short enough that I don't mind" (`10263515783`); *"새로운 루틴을 '추가'할 때만 전면 광고를 견디면 루틴을 유지 관리할 때는 거슬리지 않게 되어 있어요"* / "if you tolerate the full-screen ad only when *adding* a routine, day-to-day maintenance isn't disturbed" (`11042165108`). **Interpretation: the mechanic is survivable at 5 seconds and not at 30. The corpus contains the A/B result already.**

**Geography inverts here.** Ads are **2.48% of Korean reviews (15/605) but 9.20% of non-Korean (16/174)** — a 3.7× difference. The US alone contributes 12 of the 47 (`ads_complaint` is 15.15% of US reviews).

### 3.3.2 Widget defects — 90 reviews (11.55%, high-priority, mean ★ 3.689)

The widget is the app's marquee feature (§2.3) and its largest failure surface.

| Defect | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Window | Character |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bug_widget_not_showing` | 50 | 6.42% | high-priority | 3.440 | 2020-04-12 → 2026-07-26 | Widget absent from the gallery, blank, or black |
| `bug_widget_not_updating` | 25 | 3.21% | very strong | 4.080 | 2019-10-14 → 2026-08-27 | New habits never appear; check-ins never reflect |
| `bug_widget_group_deleted` | 9 | 1.16% | meaningful | 3.556 | 2024-04-29 → 2025-12-29 | A live group renders as "deleted" in widget config |
| `bug_widget_layout` | 6 | 0.77% | emerging | 4.333 | 2019-08-28 → 2021-05-27 | Items clipped below the widget frame |
| **Union** | **90** | **11.55%** | high-priority | 3.689 | 2019-08-28 → 2026-08-27 | 1★ 11 · 2★ 6 · 3★ 15 · 4★ 26 · **5★ 32** |

**Note the rating distribution: 58 of 90 widget-defect reviews are 4★ or 5★.** These are loyal users reporting a fault, not detractors. That is why the widget problem is invisible in the star average and very visible in the text.

**It is chronic, not episodic.** `bug_widget_not_showing` runs at 3.89% of E1 reviews → 6.67% of E2 → **10.80% of E3**. It is the only large defect theme that gets steadily worse. *"업데이트만 하면 잘되던게 꼭 뭐하나씩 안돼요. 제발 그냥 건들지 말고 가만히 놔둬주세요"* / "every update breaks something that worked. Please just leave it alone" (`11234179720`, KR, 1★); *"이제 고작 한3달째 사용중인데 위젯이 벽돌상태가 되는 경우가 너무 많아서 짜증이 나네요.. 적당히 해야지 한달에 5~6번은 새로 깔아야 하고"* / "only three months in and the widget bricks so often — I have to reinstall five or six times a month" (`11241868525`, KR, 1★).

**The "group deleted" defect is well-bounded and recent** — all 9 reviews fall between April 2024 and December 2025, and 3 of the 9 reviewers state they are Premium (`11243086221`, `13567154644` JP, `12069773156` describes the exact reproduction). *"위젯 편집에서 그룹을 선택 했더니 삭제된 그룹이라고 나옵니다"* / "I picked a group in widget edit and it says the group was deleted" (`11214649198`, KR, 1★); *"プレミアムに課金したのですが、ウィジェットのGroup選択ができません… ウィジェットの編集欄にall activitiesとしか表示されません"* / "I paid for Premium but can't select a group in the widget — only 'all activities' shows" (`13567154644`, JP, 2★).

### 3.3.3 Widget design limits — 39 reviews, separate from the defects

These are not faults; they are choices reviewers disagree with.

| Theme | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Window |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `widget_size_count_limits` — only 3/6/14 slots; too large; wasted grey cells | 23 | 2.95% | meaningful | 4.652 | 2020-10-05 → 2026-05-04 |
| `widget_tap_opens_app` — tapping the widget launches the app instead of checking in | 16 | 2.05% | meaningful | **4.750** | 2020-11-04 → 2023-12-30 |
| `widget_order_random` — widget order does not match in-app order | 6 | 0.77% | emerging | 4.333 | 2022-01-19 → 2024-04-14 |
| `widget_group_selection_request` — let the widget show one group | 4 | 0.51% | emerging | 4.000 | 2020-11-12 → 2025-12-29 |

**`widget_tap_opens_app` is a textbook regression story with a clean beginning and end.** It appears the week of the iOS 14 widget rewrite (November 2020) and stops dead after December 2023 — **zero occurrences in 2024, 2025 or 2026** — which is consistent with it having been fixed. All 16 reviewers are Korean and the mean is 4.750: they were annoyed, not angry. *"마 위젯이란 ... 위젯이면 그 화면에서만 체크가 되어야지 와 앱이 실행되노"* / "look, if it's a widget the check should happen right there — why is the app launching?" (`6613303442`); *"위젯에서 체크하는 거 돌려줘요 ⸜(`^´)⸝"* / "give us back checking from the widget" (`10138612858`); *"업데이트 이후 체크시 앱으로 들어가는게 너무 불편 하네요 그동안 잘 사용해 왔는데 안타깝네요 그동안 잘 사용해 왔음으로 별5개 드립니다"* / "since the update, checking in opens the app and it's very inconvenient. I've used it well until now, so I'm giving five stars anyway" (`6613226666` — **the clearest single illustration of warning 10 in the corpus**).

### 3.3.4 App and platform defects — 229 reviewers hit at least one

| Defect | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Window | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `update_regression` — "the update broke it" | 59 | 7.57% | high-priority | 3.729 | 2019-07-28 → 2026-05-04 | Present in every year; 53/59 Korean |
| `bug_watch_loading` — Apple Watch stuck on "Loading…" / black | 26 | 3.34% | very strong | 3.577 | 2020-04-21 → 2026-04-03 | **19 of 26 in 2020–2021**; tails off after 2023 |
| `bug_keyboard_memo` — note keyboard will not appear | 22 | 2.82% | meaningful | 4.091 | 2020-09-17 → 2020-09-27 | **21 of 22 in three days.** A single release |
| `bug_app_crash` — app will not launch / crashes on save | 18 | 2.31% | meaningful | 2.889 | 2019-12-11 → 2024-06-04 | Two clusters: Jun 2020 (8), Mar–Jun 2024 (5) |
| `bug_data_loss` — records disappeared | 12 | 1.54% | meaningful | **2.250** | 2019-05-21 → 2026-04-14 | **8 of 12 since 2024.** See below |
| `bug_add_habit_fails` — the add button errors out | 9 | 1.16% | meaningful | 2.778 | 2020-09-17 → 2026-08-24 | Often entangled with the ad gate |
| `bug_display_on_weekday` — "Display on" ignores the chosen weekdays | 8 | 1.03% | meaningful | 4.375 | 2020-09-11 → 2025-06-23 | All 8 Korean; five-year-old fault |
| `bug_ui_unresponsive` | 6 | 0.77% | emerging | 3.667 | 2020-07-20 → 2023-08-03 | |
| `bug_reminder_time` | 4 | 0.51% | emerging | 3.500 | 2020-09-20 → 2020-09-23 | Single iOS 14 cluster |
| `bug_notifications` | 4 | 0.51% | emerging | 3.750 | 2019-10-09 → 2024-10-09 | |
| `bug_stats_report`, `bug_badge_count`, `bug_color_picker`, `bug_text_contrast` | 3, 2, 2, 2 | ≤0.39% | weak | — | — | |
| `bug_battery_drain`, `bug_storage_usage`, `bug_backup_restore`, `bug_report_crash`, `bug_misc` | 1,1,1,1,5 | ≤0.64% | weak/emerging | — | — | `11805553961` (JP): *"this app uses over 10GB of my storage"* |

**`bug_data_loss` deserves promotion above its 1.54% share, under the severe-harm exception.** Twelve reviewers describe losing tracked history: `4184572783`, `6086077854`, `7437783396`, `10740823617` (MX, *"The app is amazing but all of my data got erased!"*), `11074355880`, `11222810326`, `11343272903`, `12431372182`, `12513675383`, `12570234049`, `12676089427`, `13958683782`. **Eight of the twelve are from 2024 onward and six are 1★.** *"오늘 업데이트 되면서 이전 데이터들 다 날라갔어요...이전 데이터 복구가 어려우면 업데이트 전에 미리 알림을 주던가 해야하는 거 아닌가요?"* / "today's update wiped all my previous data. If recovery is impossible, shouldn't you warn people before the update?" (`12431372182`, KR, 1★); *"습관 중 하나 횟수 조정하고 저장했는데 다른 습관 체크인해놓은거 다 한 번 한걸로 변경됐어요"* / "I adjusted the count on one habit, saved, and every other habit's check-ins collapsed to one" (`13958683782`, KR, 1★, Apr 2026). **The compounding factor is §2.2: backup is Premium-gated**, so the free users most likely to lose data are the ones least able to recover it — stated outright by `11222810326`: *"결과적으로 프리미엄 유료가 아니면 백업이나 복구가 불가하다는걸 첨 알았습니다"* / "in the end I learned for the first time that without paid Premium, backup and recovery are impossible."

### 3.3.5 Usability and discoverability — 71 reviewers (9.11%, high-priority)

| Theme | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cant_find_delete_edit` | 27 | 3.47% | very strong | 3.630 | 23 Korean, 3 Canadian, 1 US |
| `onboarding_no_guidance` | 23 | 2.95% | meaningful | 4.348 | 18 KR, 4 JP, 1 SA |
| `past_date_checkin_difficulty` | 13 | 1.67% | meaningful | 4.000 | Concentrated 2019–2020; later reviewers praise the same feature |
| `checkin_friction` | 5 | 0.64% | emerging | 4.200 | |
| `localization_gap` | 5 | 0.64% | emerging | 3.200 | JP 2, CN 1, ES 1, KR 1 |
| `usability_negative` (generic "hard to use / bad UI") | 5 | 0.64% | emerging | 2.200 | |
| `accessibility_concern` | 1 | 0.13% | weak | 4.000 | Dark mode behind a paywall vs photosensitivity |

**`cant_find_delete_edit` is the cheapest fix in the report.** Twenty-seven people could not work out how to remove a habit. *"How on earth can you make a habit app and not be able to delete the habits after entering them? I've tried pressing every menu and holding down on the habits and there's no option to delete one. It's like basic 101 of list apps"* (`8773928024`, US, 1★); *"습관 삭제 도대체 어떻게 하는거예요? 설정들어가도 안되고 빡쳐서 지웠어요"* / "how on earth do you delete a habit? It's not in settings. I got angry and deleted the app" (`7514522692`, KR, 1★); *"항목들을 간단히 지우는 기능이 없어 조금 화나요. 핸드폰을 던질 뻔 했어요"* / "there's no simple way to delete items and it makes me angry. I almost threw my phone" (`12665002271`, KR, 2★). **The function exists** — `7781598096` (KR, 5★) documents it publicly for other reviewers — **which makes this purely a discoverability defect.** Its mean of 3.630 and the fact that 19 of 27 are 3★ or better means most of these users stayed; they just wrote a bad review on the way.

**`past_date_checkin_difficulty` is a solved problem, and the corpus proves it.** All but two of its 13 reviews are from 2019–2020 (*"So if you forget to track something you're just out of luck"* — `4901780444`, US, 2019). By 2020–2022 the opposite theme appears: **`past_date_checkin_praise`, 4 reviews, mean ★ 5.000**, including *"Compared to other apps, it's ridiculously easy to add data for previous dates"* (`6522788831`, US) and *"I love that I can come back to previous day if I forget to tick something"* (`9179962294`, GB). **Interpretation: this is the one confirmed example in the corpus of a top complaint being fixed and turning into a differentiator.**

### 3.3.6 Support and the developer relationship — 15 reviewers (1.93%, meaningful, mean ★ 2.333)

| Theme | n | % | Mean ★ | IDs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `support_unresponsive` — wrote in, got nothing | 8 | 1.03% | **2.000** | `6173955532`, `6475747339`, `8287496836`, `10513784132`, `11222810326`, `12128022974`, `13840622066`, `14185972618` |
| `support_channel_unclear` — could not find how to reach anyone | 6 | 0.77% | 2.833 | `6438394097`, `7480839830`, `9955688255`, `11814795489`, `12805909201`, `13014831178` |
| `support_dismissive` — got a reply that denied their experience | 1 | 0.13% | 2.000 | `12570234049` |
| `roadmap_promise_unmet` — told "planned", never shipped | 6 | 0.77% | 4.000 | `11361971544`, `11505457261`, `11832774003`, `12389229520`, `13540816428`, `13840622066` |
| **`support_responsive_praise`** — the counter-evidence | **5** | 0.64% | **5.000** | `3971282028`, `5621508960`, `9511684630`, `10644515708`, `11603064365` |

**Both sides are real and they are separated in time.** The five praise reviews run 2019–2024 and describe fast, personal replies, twice by KakaoTalk: *"카톡 문의에 대해 친절하게 안내해주셔서 정말 감사합니다"* / "thank you for the kind guidance on my KakaoTalk enquiry" (`10644515708`, 2023); *"앱 사용 질문사항 있어서 카톡 남겼는데 9시 되자마자 연락주셨네요"* / "I messaged on KakaoTalk with a question and got a reply the moment 9am hit" (`11603064365`, 2024). The eight unresponsive reviews cluster later and half are since 2024. *"I contacted the developer to report the bug, and they told me to wait, but the issue was never fixed. They also ignored my follow-up email"* (`14185972618`, CA, 1★, Jun 2026); *"카톡 문의해도 읽기만 하고 답은 없고, 돈이 아깝네요 실망입니다"* / "I asked on KakaoTalk, they just read it and never replied. What a waste of money. Disappointed" (`12128022974`, KR, 1★); *"the support link goes to some Asian industrial company"* (`12805909201`, US, 2★). **Interpretation: support capacity did not scale, and the reviewers who notice hardest are the ones who paid.**

### 3.3.7 Monetization friction — 89 reviewers (11.42%, high-priority, mean ★ 2.989)

Covered in depth in Part 5. The headline counts: `free_tier_cap` 20 (2.57%), `price_too_high` 20 (2.57%), `post_purchase_failure` 22 (2.82%), `monetization_model_change_complaint` 10 (1.28%, **mean ★ 1.700 — the lowest of any theme with n ≥ 5**), `paywall_feature_gating` 8 (1.03%), `refund_request` 8 (1.03%), `restore_purchase_failure` 8 (1.03%), `accidental_purchase` 6 (0.77%), `price_discount_request` 6 (0.77%), `subscription_objection` 4 (0.51%), `premium_not_applied` 3, `price_unbundling_request` 3, `purchase_failure` 3, `trial_request` 3, `billing_error` 2, `price_increase_complaint` 2, `reliability_blocks_purchase` 2, `misleading_premium_claims` 1, `subscription_request` 1.

## 3.4 Unmet needs — 267 reviewers (34.27%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.449)

**A third of the corpus asks for something, and they overwhelmingly ask nicely** — the request family's mean of 4.449 is above the corpus mean of 4.134. These are not complaints; they are a seven-year roadmap written by the user base.

| Request | n | % of 779 | Signal | Mean ★ | Window | Status per the corpus |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `req_icloud_sync` — sync across devices | **44** | 5.65% | high-priority | 4.318 | 2019-05-11 → 2026-04-02 | 1 reviewer reports it working (2024, JP); 6 KR reviewers after that say it doesn't |
| `req_multiple_checkins_day` — log a habit n times a day | **24** | 3.08% | very strong | 4.167 | 2019-04-24 → 2026-05-05 | Partially shipped as "goal"; then **capped at 5 and tier-gated** |
| `req_ipad_version` — native iPad layout | **21** | 2.70% | meaningful | 4.667 | 2019-04-01 → 2026-07-12 | Never shipped; 6 reviewers say they were told it was planned |
| `req_widget_improvements` — widget options generally | 20 | 2.57% | meaningful | 4.700 | 2019-03-31 → 2024-05-17 | |
| `req_backup_export` | 17 | 2.18% | meaningful | 4.176 | 2019-04-02 → 2026-05-03 | Dropbox shipped Jan 2020; still manual and Premium-gated |
| `req_weekday_display_filter` — show only today's habits | 16 | 2.05% | meaningful | 4.250 | 2019-09-06 → 2024-03-05 | Shipped as "Display on" — **and 8 reviewers report it not working** |
| `req_android_version` | 13 | 1.67% | meaningful | 4.615 | 2020-06-17 → 2025-03-04 | All 13 Korean. Not shipped |
| `req_ui_tweak` (misc. layout asks) | 13 | 1.67% | meaningful | 4.385 | 2019-10-12 → 2025-08-26 | |
| `req_past_report_history` — see last week's/month's report | 11 | 1.41% | meaningful | 4.364 | 2020-01-31 → 2025-06-20 | Still current in 2025 |
| `req_memo_on_failed_day` — write a note on a day you missed | 9 | 1.16% | meaningful | 4.556 | 2019-07-26 → 2025-09-13 | Requested for six straight years; not shipped |
| `req_photo_attachment` | 9 | 1.16% | meaningful | 4.333 | 2019-07-09 → 2024-07-29 | Shipped but capped at 3 |
| `req_mac_version` | 8 | 1.03% | meaningful | 4.500 | 2022-01-03 → 2025-03-26 | All 8 Korean |
| `req_interval_scheduling` — every-N-days, monthly, yearly, month-end | 7 | 0.90% | emerging | 3.571 | 2020-01-16 → 2026-08-23 | |
| `req_skip_rest_day` | 7 | 0.90% | emerging | 4.857 | 2020-12-06 → 2026-04-16 | **Shipped mid-2024** (`11410069153` thanks the dev) — but still requested after |
| `req_todo_oneoff_tasks` | 7 | 0.90% | emerging | 4.714 | 2020-05-06 → 2024-12-28 | |
| `req_archive_history` — keep the record when archiving | 6 | 0.77% | emerging | 4.500 | 2019-06-03 → 2025-08-26 | |
| 12 requests at n = 4–5 | — | — | emerging | — | — | reorder/sort, partial-completion mark, multiple reminders, watch support, calendar view, goal %, memo aggregate, more colours, widget calendar, group selection, dark mode, journaling |
| 29 requests at n = 1 | — | 0.13% each | weak | — | — | Enumerated by ID at §9.2 / §9.3. **"One person said this."** |

**Three requests deserve special treatment because they are about capabilities that partly exist:**

1. **Multiple check-ins per day (24 requests, 2019–2026).** Asked for continuously from `4054464741` (Apr 2019: *"약 챙겨먹기 처럼 하루에 세번 네번 이렇게 스탬프 찍는 것도 추가 해줄 수 있을까요?"* / "could you add stamping three or four times a day, like for medication?") to `14031614226` (May 2026). It was **shipped** as "goal"/"daily check-in" (`12095285181`: *"하루에 3번씩 체크해야 하는 항목을 goal을 통해 설정할 수 있다는 장점"*), then **capped at 5** (`11315669095` JP: *"Why is 5 times per day the maximum? I need 10"*), then **restricted by tier** — producing the three angriest change complaints in the corpus (`13169190954` JP, `13386826119` SG, `12570234049` KR). **Interpretation: this is the same request being answered, then partially withdrawn, over seven years.**

2. **Per-weekday display (16 requests + 8 defect reports = 24 reviewers).** Shipped as "Display on". Eight reviewers say it does not work — *"몇번을 편집하고 삭제하고 다시 해도 display on 기능이 안되네요. 일주일에 3회 화목토만 뜨게 체크했는데 전 일자에 전부 떠요"* / "no matter how many times I edit and recreate it, Display on doesn't work — I set Tue/Thu/Sat and it shows every day" (`11318555804`, KR, 3★); *"Display on을 설정해도 매일 매일 떠요. 처음에는 안 그랬는데"* / "even with Display on set it shows every day. It didn't used to" (`9672617646`, KR, 4★). **A shipped feature that silently fails is worse than a missing one: the request keeps arriving and the fix never lands.**

3. **Notes on a missed day (9 requests, 2019–2025).** The app only lets you write a note after a successful check-in. Reviewers want the opposite. *"할 일을 못한 날 왜 못했는지 적고 싶은데"* / "I want to write down why I couldn't do it on the days I failed" (`7474862382`, KR, 5★); *"실패사유를 못 적어서 아쉽더라구요"* (`13130410310`, 2025); *"Would you able to enable memo added feature for non checked-in item? Sometime like we can add in the reason for didn't checked it in as future reference"* (`7765160326`, MY). **Interpretation: this is a small feature with unusually consistent wording across six years and three languages — the sign of a real gap rather than a stray idea.**

## 3.5 Positive themes in detail

### 3.5.1 UX praise — 198 reviews (25.42%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.753)

`simplicity_minimal` 95 (12.20%, mean 4.779) · `ease_of_use` 81 (10.40%, mean **4.901**) · `aesthetic_design` 69 (8.86%, mean 4.609) · `color_customization_praise` 22 (2.82%) · `font_options_praise` 2 (0.26%).

The phrasing is remarkably stable across seven years and every language in the corpus. *"군더더기없이 깔끔하고"* / "clean with no fat" (`3948640864`, 2019, the corpus's first Korean review) → *"모든 것이 과하지않고 너무너무 깔끔하고"* / "nothing is excessive, so very clean" (`12794891062`, 2025). *"It's so simple to use and straightforward. The layout also makes it easy to see the frequency of activities. … It's less complicated compared to other apps with too many features"* (`6262697602`, SG); *"Es minimalista y muy útil"* / "it's minimalist and very useful" (`9423831917`, MX); *"Easy to use and cute design"* (`8049708812`, DE).

**Importantly, `simplicity_minimal` is the only major praise theme that declines steeply** — 14.77% of E1 → 12.22% of E2 → **7.51% of E3**. `ease_of_use` (10.36 → 11.11 → 9.86) and `aesthetic_design` (9.33 → 7.22 → 9.39) are flat. **Interpretation: people still find it easy and still find it pretty. They have stopped calling it clean.** The most plausible in-corpus explanation is that the ad gate and the paywall are now part of the experience being described.

### 3.5.2 Competitive displacement — 69 reviews (8.86%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.884)

The strongest *quality* signal in the corpus, because it is comparative. 69 reviewers position this app against alternatives they personally tried. In Korean the recurring self-description is *유목민* ("nomad") and *정착* ("settled"). *"앱 여러개 써보다가 이걸로 선택하고 유료 구입했어요"* / "I tried several apps, chose this one and bought it" (`10489526756`); *"습관 앱 전부 써봤는데 이게 제일 좋음"* / "I've tried every habit app; this is the best" (`10509051172`); *"다른거 써봐도 돌아돌아 여기로 와요"* / "I try others and keep circling back here" (`8772322659`); *"직관적으로 깔끔한 덕분에 2시간 넘게 어플 깔고 지우다 겨우 정착합니다 습관 어플 중 제한 없이 통계 제공하는 앱 중 최고에요"* / "after two hours of installing and deleting apps I finally settled here — the best among habit apps that give unrestricted statistics" (`7176051984`).

**A separate, smaller signal cuts the other way: `competitor_feature_gap`, 5 reviews (0.64%), mean ★ 5.000** — reviewers who name a rival that has something DayStamp lacks. `4949407944` (Apple Health: *"Streak나 Habit Miner 같은 습관앱은 지원하는데"* / "habit apps like Streak and Habit Miner support it"); `8157214041` (per-habit motivation note: *"비슷한 어플인 donhabit이란 어플이 있는데 그 어플에는 그게 있더라고요"*); `10969192626` (incomplete-day marking — *"달력에 미완료 표시만 가능하게 넣어주신다면 당장 유료결제하겠습니다!!!!"* / "add incomplete-day marking to the calendar and I'll pay immediately"); `13538382083` (US, journaling: *"add a section for general journaling as well to compete with the other apps"*); `6683329124` (time tracking — says they run a second app for it). **All five are five-star reviewers telling you exactly what would make them pay or consolidate.**

### 3.5.3 Named-feature praise — 135 reviews (17.33%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.785)

`widget_praise` 30 (3.85%) · `stats_reports_praise` 24 (3.08%) · `memo_note_praise` 21 (2.70%) · `stamp_satisfaction` 19 (2.44%) · `calendar_view_praise` 11 (1.41%) · `reminders_praise` 10 (1.28%) · `watch_praise` 8 (1.03%) · `grouping_praise` 7 (0.90%) · `feature_shipped_praise` 7 (0.90%) · `sound_feedback_praise` 5 (0.64%) · `past_date_checkin_praise` 4 (0.51%) · `non_punitive_praise` 3 (0.39%) · `goal_count_praise`, `backup_added_praise`, `icloud_sync_added_praise` 1 each.

**`stamp_satisfaction` (19 reviews, mean ★ 4.947) is the app's emotional core and it is specific to this product's metaphor.** *"도장이 꽉꽉 찍히는 것만큼 직관적이고 효율적인 보상체계도 잘 없을 거예요"* / "there's hardly a reward system more intuitive and efficient than stamps filling up" (`3968135443`); *"매일 도장 쿵쿵 찍으니 재미도 있고"* / "stamping every day is actually fun" (`5915290653`); *"캘린더에 도트 예쁘게 채우고 싶어서 뭔가 자꾸 하고싶어져요"* / "I want to fill the calendar with pretty dots so I keep wanting to do things" (`6437355632`). `sound_feedback_praise` (5) is the same mechanism in audio: *"습관 체크할때나는 소리에 약간의 희열감 있어서"* / "there's a little thrill in the sound when you check a habit" (`6842138669`).

**`non_punitive_praise` (3 reviews) is small but strategically loud**, because it is the one thing reviewers say this app does that streak-based competitors do not. *"This is the only habit-building app that 1) didn't make me feel like a failure for missing a day / breaking a streak and 2) had the few specific features I wanted"* (`8193352845`, US, 5★); *"there's no penalty or annoying reminder to do the goal, but when you check it off it gives you a big pop up with confetti and it gives me ALL the dopamine"* (`10887501928`, US, 5★); *"really appreciate its clean design and calm, non-judgmental approach to habit tracking"* (`13574542005`, RU, 4★).

### 3.5.4 Outcomes and advocacy — 43 + 24 reviews

`outcome_behavior_change` **43 (5.52%, high-priority, mean ★ 4.930)** — reviewers who state a behaviour change, not an intention. *"데이스탬프로 인생이 바뀌고 있다"* / "DayStamp is changing my life" (`5604731898`); *"덕분에 5개월 넘게 좋은습관들 이어 나가고 있습니다"* / "thanks to this I've kept good habits going for over five months" (`7062715389`); *"I have set a dozen mini goals so that no matter what kind of day I am having, I can do one or two mini goals and feel successful"* (`4280559577`, US); *"게을러서 습관 만들기 넘무 힘들었는데 정말 도움이 많이됬어요"* / "I'm lazy and building habits was so hard, but this helped a lot" (`8395300492`). **It falls sharply over time: 7.25% of E1 → 5.56% of E2 → 2.35% of E3.**

`advocacy_recommend` **24 (3.08%, mean ★ 4.917)** and `first_review_ever` **7 (0.90%, mean ★ 5.000)** — people who never write reviews, writing one. *"원래 배민 리뷰는 써도 어플 리뷰는 안남기는 사람인데,, 너무 좋아서 리뷰 쓰러 직접 앱스토어에 찾아 왔습니다"* / "I write food-delivery reviews but never app reviews — this was so good I came to the App Store specially" (`6861085361`); *"I've never written a review for an app before but i love this app SO much and i want everyone in the world to know about it"* (`10887501928`, US).

`developer_appreciation` **30 (3.85%, mean ★ 4.933)** — almost entirely Korean (27/30), and several address the developer by name: *"필수앱! 동현개발자님 응원합니다-!!!!"* / "essential app! Cheering for developer Donghyun!" (`7004263966`); *"개발자분들 복 받으세요"* / "may the developers be blessed" (`5947186533`); *"진짜 개발자님 돈방석 앉으세요"* / "developer, may you sit on a cushion of money" (`6842138669`).

## 3.6 Acquisition signals — thin, and that is itself a finding

Only **2 reviewers in 779 name how they found the app**: `13560296828` (KR, 2025, *"제가 원하는 기능으로 앱 추천을 gpt로 물어봐서 알게됐어요"* / "I found it by asking GPT to recommend an app with the features I wanted" — the corpus's only AI-discovery attribution) and `6861085361` (KR, 2021, *"인터넷서핑하다 발견하자마자 바로 깔았는데"* / "found it while browsing and installed immediately"). One more notes the app's obscurity: *"No puedo creer que tenga tan pocas descargas"* / "I can't believe it has so few downloads" (`12018975480`, MX).

**Interpretation: unlike creator-led apps, this corpus contains essentially no channel attribution. Growth appears to be organic App Store discovery plus Korean word of mouth (24 advocacy reviews). That is a finding about the data, not proof about the channel mix — but it does mean this corpus cannot tell you where users come from.**

---

# PART 4 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

**Distribution:** 5★ 450 (57.77%) · 4★ 156 (20.03%) · 3★ 72 (9.24%) · 2★ 29 (3.72%) · 1★ 72 (9.24%) · mean **4.134**.

**Before reading any of this, apply warning 10.** 162 of the 450 five-star reviews (36.00% of that segment) carry at least one negative-direction theme. In this corpus — and especially in its Korean majority — **a five-star rating frequently means "I like this app" and the body means "and here is what is broken".** Every one of the 72 one-star reviews, by contrast, carries a negative theme; the bottom of the scale is unambiguous and the top is not.

## 4.1 Five stars (n = 450, 57.77%) — mean body 75 characters

| Theme | n | % of the 450 |
|---|---|---|
| `simplicity_minimal` | 76 | 16.9% |
| `ease_of_use` | 73 | 16.2% |
| `comparative_best` | 62 | 13.8% |
| `paid_premium_confirmed` | 52 | 11.6% |
| `aesthetic_design` | 49 | 10.9% |
| `outcome_behavior_change` | 40 | 8.9% |
| **`question_to_developer`** | **35** | **7.8%** |
| `feature_completeness_praise` | 32 | 7.1% |
| **`req_icloud_sync`** | **29** | **6.4%** |
| `developer_appreciation` | 28 | 6.2% |
| **`update_regression`** | **26** | **5.8%** |
| `stats_reports_praise` / `widget_praise` / `advocacy_recommend` | 22 each | 4.9% |

**Why people give five stars:** the app is clean, easy, prettier and simpler than the alternatives they tried, and it made them do the thing. **What they put in the body anyway:** a question for the developer (35), a sync request (29), a regression report (26), a widget defect (32 across the four widget-defect themes).

**The five-star complaint is the corpus's signature form.** *"정말 잘 사용하고 있는데 진짜 정말로 아쉬운거 딱하나,,, 동기화 시켜주십쇼"* / "I really am using it well, just one genuinely disappointing thing — please give us sync" (`8942549944`, 5★); *"근데 갑자기 위젯이 그룹이 삭제되었다고 떠요ㅜㅜ 다시 만들어도 계속 그래용.."* / "using it well but suddenly the widget says my group was deleted" (`12304194492`, 5★); *"잘쓰고 있다가 하루만에.."* ⁄ *"위젯에 먼쓸리 띄워놨는데, 다음날부터 위젯 창에 까만 화면만 나오네요.. 그 기능 하나 보고 산건데,,,, … 조치해주시면 별점 수정하겠습니다."* / "was using it fine and after one day the widget shows only a black screen. I bought it for that one feature. Fix it and I'll change my rating" (`13014831178` — **filed at 1★, and explicitly offering to raise it**).

## 4.2 Four stars (n = 156, 20.03%)

| Theme | n | % of the 156 |
|---|---|---|
| `paid_premium_confirmed` | 28 | 17.9% |
| `simplicity_minimal` | 17 | 10.9% |
| `aesthetic_design` / `bug_widget_not_showing` / `question_to_developer` | 14 each | 9.0% |
| `update_regression` | 12 | 7.7% |
| `cant_find_delete_edit` | 10 | 6.4% |
| `ease_of_use` / `bug_widget_not_updating` | 8 each | 5.1% |

**Four stars is the payer's bucket.** At 17.9%, `paid_premium_confirmed` is the single most common theme here — payers with one unresolved problem. The four-star review is structurally "I paid, I like it, and one specific thing is wrong": *"프리미엄으로 잘 사용하고 있습니다 ... 흰배경으로 설정 시 글자가 그대로 흰색이더라고요"* / "I'm using Premium happily … but with a white background the text stays white" (`11159431115`); *"Happily enjoying the paid version of this beautiful and useful app … Is it at all possible to be able to have unlimited widget options?"* (`10678560230`, US).

## 4.3 Three stars (n = 72, 9.24%)

| Theme | n | % of the 72 |
|---|---|---|
| `update_regression` | 9 | 12.5% |
| `bug_widget_not_showing` | 8 | 11.1% |
| **`price_too_high`** | **6** | **8.3%** |
| `aesthetic_design` / `ads_gate_habit_creation` | 5 each | 6.9% |
| `intent_to_purchase` / `bug_widget_not_updating` / `bug_watch_loading` / `ads_complaint` / `free_tier_cap` | 4 each | 5.6% |

**Three stars is where price lives.** `price_too_high` is 8.3% of three-star reviews against 2.57% of the corpus — a 3.2× concentration. This is the "I would pay but not that much" bucket, and it contains the unbundling proposals (`10639075443` PL: *"adding a monthly payment plan would be so useful. The lifetime premium plan is not at all affordable to a wider audience"*) and the discount requests (`10850332771` KR, `10683827705` MX: *"I want to purchase the app, please put a discount on it 🥹"*).

## 4.4 Two stars (n = 29, 3.72%) — the smallest and most informative bucket

| Theme | n | % of the 29 |
|---|---|---|
| `ads_complaint` | 5 | 17.2% |
| `bug_widget_not_showing` / `bug_watch_loading` / `ads_gate_habit_creation` | 4 each | 13.8% |
| `update_regression` / `monetization_model_change_complaint` / `req_multiple_checkins_day` / `bug_keyboard_memo` / `req_icloud_sync` / `long_tenure_user` / `churn_uninstalled` | 3 each | 10.3% |

**Two stars is where long-tenure users land when the terms change under them.** `long_tenure_user` is 10.3% of this bucket against 2.95% globally, and `monetization_model_change_complaint` is 10.3% against 1.28% — an **8× concentration**. All three of the "it used to work this way" reviews are here: `13386826119` (SG, years-long user, daily-goal interval removed), `13169190954` (JP, daily check-in became paid), `12570234049` (KR, *"출시 직후부터 계속 잘 사용해오고 있는 유저입니다"* / "I've used this well since right after launch", whose 2× check-ins were retroactively collapsed to 1× and whose support reply denied that this was possible). **Interpretation: this bucket is the price of silent tier changes, paid by the app's oldest users.**

## 4.5 One star (n = 72, 9.24%) — mean body 61 characters

| Theme | n | % of the 72 |
|---|---|---|
| **`churn_uninstalled`** | **20** | **27.8%** |
| **`ads_complaint`** | **18** | **25.0%** |
| `paid_premium_confirmed` | 15 | 20.8% |
| **`post_purchase_failure`** | **13** | **18.1%** |
| **`ads_gate_habit_creation`** | **13** | **18.1%** |
| `update_regression` / `bug_widget_not_showing` | 9 each | 12.5% |
| `bug_app_crash` | 7 | 9.7% |
| `free_tier_cap` / `bug_data_loss` | 6 each | 8.3% |
| `support_unresponsive` / `monetization_model_change_complaint` / `cant_find_delete_edit` | 5 each | 6.9% |

**The one-star review has exactly two authors in this corpus.**

**(a) The new user who hit the ad gate and left** — 20 of 72 (27.8%) state they uninstalled, and 18 of 72 (25.0%) blame ads. These reviews are short (median 61 characters), fast and final. They arrive before the user has seen a single feature.

**(b) The paying customer whose purchase stopped working** — **15 of the 72 one-star reviews (20.8%) come from self-identified payers**, and 13 (18.1%) report a post-purchase failure. This is a startling ratio: payers are 12.71% of the corpus but 20.8% of its one-star reviews. *"이럴거면 유료구매왜함"* / "why did I even pay" (`14351907761`); *"유료 버전 구매했는데 위젯도 안되고 한번 만든 습관도 수정 안돼서 카톡 문의해도 읽기만 하고 답은 없고, 돈이 아깝네요"* / "bought the paid version, the widget doesn't work, I can't edit a habit I made, and KakaoTalk support just reads and doesn't reply. Waste of money" (`12128022974`).

**These two authors need opposite fixes.** (a) is a positioning and pacing problem. (b) is an engineering and support problem. Treating one-star reviews as a single population would produce the wrong roadmap.

## 4.6 Rating / text contradictions — disclosed, not dropped

Four reviews are explicitly contradictory and are tagged `rating_text_contradiction` (0.51%, weak):

| ID | Store | ★ | What makes it contradictory |
|---|---|---|---|
| `6613226666` | KR | **5** | A complaint about a widget regression, with the reviewer stating they are giving 5★ *because of past service*: *"그동안 잘 사용해 왔음으로 별5개 드립니다"* |
| `7423763648` | KR | **3** | Body is *"정말 좋아요👍"* / "really great 👍" with no criticism at all |
| `10245972595` | HK | **3** | Body is unambiguously delighted: *"After a long time of going through tons of habit tracking apps, finally got the app I wanted! And got all my needed features for free :)"* |
| `13299429338` | US | **2** | *"I absolutely love this app and I want to give it 5 stars, but the weekly widgets NEVER work"* |

**The broader pattern is not tagged because it is too large to be an anomaly — it is the norm.** 162 five-star reviews carry a negative theme (36.00% of the 5★ segment). **Interpretation: in this corpus the star rating measures loyalty and the body measures product state. They should be read as two different instruments, and Part 3 is built on the body.**

---

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 Who is identifiable as a payer

**99 reviewers (12.71% of 779, high-priority) state they purchased Premium.** This is the largest directly-identifiable payer cohort in this dataset's habit-tracker corpora and it is the analytical centre of this report.

| Property | Value |
|---|---|
| n | **99 (12.71% of 779)** |
| Mean ★ | **4.010** (corpus 4.134 — payers rate *lower* than the corpus) |
| Rating split | 5★ 52 · 4★ 28 · 3★ 2 · 2★ 2 · **1★ 15** |
| Storefronts | kr 78 · us 11 · jp 4 · ca 2 · mx 2 · es 1 · hk 1 |
| By year | 2019: 4 · 2020: 17 · 2021: 10 · 2022: 12 · 2023: 10 · **2024: 27** · 2025: 7 · **2026: 12 (32.4% of that year's 37 reviews)** |
| By era | E1 (2019–21) 31, mean 4.258 · E2 (2022–23) 22, mean 4.091 · **E3 (2024–26) 46, mean 3.804** |

**Boundary rule:** `paid_premium_confirmed` requires the reviewer to state they bought it. It **excludes** the 6 `accidental_purchase` reviewers who paid by mistake and wanted a refund (`3982059993`, `5329161510`, `6288381604`, `6824956220`, `6829894423`, `9462337168`) — they are payers in the billing sense but not in the intent sense, and mixing them would corrupt the satisfaction figures. They are analysed separately in §5.6.

**This is not a conversion rate.** 12.71% is the share of *written reviews* mentioning payment. Silent payers and silent free users are both invisible.

## 5.2 What triggers a purchase — in buyers' own words

Thirty reviewers state an intention to buy (`intent_to_purchase`, 3.85%, mean ★ 4.300), and many payers narrate the moment. The triggers, ranked by how often they appear in that text:

**(a) A short free trial period of self-directed use — usually days, sometimes one.**
*"하루 사용해보고 너무 좋아서 바로 유료로 구매했어요"* / "used it for one day, loved it, bought it immediately" (`6437355632`); *"하루 써보고 유료결제 했습니다!"* (`7585307830`); *"발견하자마자 편리하고 좋은거같아서 프리미엄 구매했어요"* / "the moment I found it, it seemed convenient and good, so I bought Premium" (`5714521465`); *"한달만 더 사용해보고 (계속 쓸 것 같지만 )계속쓰겠다 싶으면 전체기록 보유하고 싶으니 유료구매할거에요"* / "one more month and if I'm still using it I'll buy, because I want to keep the full record" (`6181430289`).

**(b) Removing ads.** *"광고제거때문에 프리미엄 결제하고싶은데"* / "I want to buy Premium just to remove the ads" (`8235594886`); *"Paying for Premium is a definite must have - ads are extremely bothersome without it"* (`8751577977`, US).

**(c) A specific gated feature they have already decided they need** — most often the weekly/calendar widget or backup. *"일주일이 한눈에 보이는 위젯이 좋아서 오늘부터 구매해서 사용중입니다"* / "I liked the week-at-a-glance widget so I bought it today" (`14057836658`); *"This app advertised a weekly visual habit widget as part of the lifetime premium purchase"* (`14185972618`, CA); *"위젯에 먼쓸리 띄워놨는데 … 그 기능 하나 보고 산건데"* / "I put the monthly widget up … I bought it for that one feature" (`13014831178`).

**(d) Gratitude and support, with no feature attached at all.** *"잘 만들어주셔서 프리미엄도 구매했네요ㅎㅎ"* / "you made a good app, so I bought Premium" (`5611409056`); *"Just bought the lifetime premium - I support the developers and kudos on doing such a great job y'all!!!!"* (`8419383942`, CA); *"구매 옵션도 구독형이 아니라 일회성 결제이기 때문에 조만간 감사의 의미로 지불할 예정입니다"* / "the purchase option is one-time rather than subscription, so I intend to pay soon as a gesture of thanks" (`11042165108`, US, written in Korean); *"당근 유료 구매했고 내돈내산 리뷰입니다"* / "of course I paid; this is an I-bought-it-myself review" (`8672688722`).

**(e) The lifetime model itself.** `lifetime_purchase_praise`, **8 reviews (1.03%, mean ★ 4.875)**, and it is the purchase reason, not a footnote. *"Love the idea of lifetime subscription. Premium version is simple and great"* (`7106407718`, US); *"The one time fee for the premium version is also very worth it"* (`7678866168`, US); *"Better than the apps that charge hundreds of dollars a year"* (`8100714507`, US); *"Otro punto muy positivo es que el pago es único sin ningún tipo de suscripción… me parece abusivo que pretendan que les pagues cada mes"* / "another very positive point is that payment is one-off with no subscription… charging monthly for a simple calendar or diary seems abusive to me" (`6636232220`, ES — who then bought the full version). **6 of the 8 are non-Korean, and 4 are US.**

## 5.3 What paid users value after buying

Among the 99 payers (segment denominator 99, labelled as a segment rate throughout):

| Theme | n | % of payers | Global n |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ease_of_use` | 11 | 11.11% | 81 |
| `simplicity_minimal` | 10 | 10.10% | 95 |
| `comparative_best` | 9 | 9.09% | 69 |
| `aesthetic_design` | 8 | 8.08% | 69 |
| `price_fair_praise` | 6 | 6.06% | 8 |
| `widget_praise` | 6 | 6.06% | 30 |
| `lifetime_purchase_praise` | 5 | 5.05% | 8 |
| `todo_list_use` | 4 | 4.04% | 8 |
| `stats_reports_praise` / `advocacy_recommend` | 4 each | 4.04% | 24 / 24 |

**`price_fair_praise` (8 global, 6 of them payers) is the counterweight to `price_too_high`.** *"전체적으로 어플이 정말 깔끔합니다 프리미엄 쓸만한 가치가 있어요"* / "the app is really clean overall; Premium is worth it" (`10791311772`); *"何か複数の習慣を徹底していきたい人にとっては4500円課金する価値あると思います"* / "for someone who wants to be thorough about multiple habits, I think ¥4,500 is worth paying" (`12332213705`, JP); *"vale la pena pagar la versión premium!"* / "the premium version is worth paying for!" (`11771136717`, MX); *"정말정말정말 돈 하나도 안아깝구요, 최고에요"* / "I don't regret the money at all, it's the best" (`6144426625`). **Interpretation: at the 2020–2022 price the value case was made easily; every `price_too_high` review from 2024 onward quotes the higher figure.**

## 5.4 Post-purchase failure — where paying customers get hurt

**This is the report's most important monetization finding. 31 of the 99 payers (31.31% of the segment) report a purchase-side failure.**

| Failure mode | n (global) | % of 779 | Mean ★ | n among payers |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `post_purchase_failure` — paid, and a paid feature does not work | 22 | 2.82% | **2.136** | 22 |
| `restore_purchase_failure` — purchase lost on reinstall / device change | 8 | 1.03% | 3.125 | 7 |
| `premium_not_applied` — payment went through, entitlement did not | 3 | 0.39% | 2.667 | 3 |
| `purchase_failure` — could not complete the purchase at all | 3 | 0.39% | 2.667 | 1 |
| `billing_error` — charged an amount they did not expect | 2 | 0.26% | 3.500 | 2 |
| `misleading_premium_claims` — the listing promised something Premium did not deliver | 1 | 0.13% | 1.000 | 1 |
| **Union** | **36** | **4.62%** | **2.528** | **31 (31.31% of payers)** |

**And it is accelerating.** Payers reporting a failure: **4 of 31 in E1 (12.9%) → 6 of 22 in E2 (27.3%) → 21 of 46 in E3 (45.7%).** Nearly half of every reviewer who says they paid in 2024–2026 also says something they paid for is broken.

The failures are concrete and repeat:
- **Entitlement not applied.** *"결제는 되었으나 적용이 안됩니다"* / "the payment went through but it isn't applied" (`5370880957`); *"구매후에도 프리미엄 적용이 안됩니다"* (`6887164010`, 1★); *"프리미엄 구매를 했는데 전에 쓰던거랑 차이가 없어요. 사진 설명에 있는 기능을 프리미엄을 구매했음에도 사용할 수가 없습니다"* / "I bought Premium and nothing differs from before — I can't use the features shown in the screenshots" (`8402874369`).
- **Restore fails on a new device or reinstall.** *"프리미엄 구매후 잘 쓰다가 휴대폰을 바꾸고 다시 동기화시켜서 쓰려고 하는데 프리미엄 구매한 것이 사라졌어요"* / "I changed phones and my Premium purchase is gone" (`11753525633`); *"결제해서 잘 쓰고있었던 것 같은데 지우고 다시 까니까 광고가 나와요"* / "I'm fairly sure I paid, but after reinstalling I get ads again" (`11551843035`); *"프리미엄 복구와 알림 정상 영업을 알려주세요 😭"* (`11814795489`).
- **A Premium feature specifically fails.** *"프리미엄 구매했는데, 습관이 5개밖에 안되요"* (`12020319439`, 1★); *"프리미엄 전용 위젯에 체크한 습관이 바로바로 업데이트가 안돼요 너무 느립니다 프리미엄 적은 돈도 아닌데"* / "the Premium-only widget doesn't update my check-ins promptly. Premium isn't cheap" (`10747044142`); *"プレミアムに課金したのですが、ウィジェットのGroup選択ができません"* (`13567154644`, JP, 2★).
- **The promise did not match.** *"I am very sad because I bought this app because in the premium option they said that it was compatible to share with family and now that I have paid it is not compatible… I do not think it is right that they lie"* (`10724795076`, MX, 1★). **The store listing does advertise Family Sharing (§2.3), which makes this reviewer's account externally plausible.**

**Refunds are rare and that is not good news. Only 8 reviewers (1.03%) request a refund, and 6 of those 8 are `accidental_purchase` reviewers from 2019–2023 who mis-tapped.** Only **two** refund requests come from genuine dissatisfaction: `9349167270` (US, 2022, widget ordering: *"I'd like his but fixed or a refund"* [sic]) and `14351975658` (KR, July 2026, after re-buying and still being broken). **Interpretation: unhappy payers overwhelmingly do not seek money back — they write a one-star review and remain broken. The absence of refund requests should not be read as satisfaction.**

## 5.5 The July 2026 Premium outage — the corpus's clearest causal chain

**Eight reviews between 24 and 28 July 2026 (5 days), six of them 1★, all describing Premium failing.** In a year with only 37 reviews, these eight are **21.6% of everything written in 2026**.

| Date | ID | Store | ★ | What it says |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-07-24 | `14340621141` | KR | 1 | *"복구하기가 왜 되지 않나요?"* / "why won't restore work?" |
| 2026-07-24 | `14340812621` | KR | 4 | *"프리미엄 구독 중인데 위젯에 표기가 안됩니다..계속 에러가 뜨는데"* / "I'm on Premium but the widget won't display — errors keep coming" |
| 2026-07-25 | `14346556200` | KR | 4 | *"구독햇는데 자꾸 오류가 떠서 위젯설정이 안되네여"* / "I subscribed but errors keep stopping widget setup" |
| 2026-07-26 | `14350226011` | KR | 1 | *"프리미엄 구독했는데 … 왜오류나냐고 뭐임"* / "I subscribed to Premium … why is it erroring, what is this" |
| 2026-07-26 | `14351907761` | KR | 1 | *"유료구매했는데 위젯설정안됨 … 이럴거면 유료구매왜함"* |
| 2026-07-26 | `14351975658` | KR | 1 | Re-bought Premium annual, still broken, asks for a refund |
| 2026-07-27 | `14352377997` | HK | 1 | *"I paid for the premium and now it asks for subscription??! DO NOT BUY IT"* |
| 2026-07-28 | `14358584372` | KR | 1 | *"3.1.1로 업뎃했는데도 안돼요 빨리 해결좀요"* / "still broken even after updating to 3.1.1, please fix fast" |

**Externally corroborated.** Publicly reported release notes for this app state that **version 3.1.0 could fail to activate Premium correctly and that 3.1.1 contains a fix for applying Premium and restoring purchases** (§2.3, §9.5 — low–medium confidence, search-engine summary only, the store page itself being egress-blocked). The internal and external accounts match on mechanism, on symptom and on version number.

**Three consequences visible in the text:**
1. **The last reviewer says 3.1.1 did not fix it for them.** Whether that is a second defect or an entitlement-cache problem cannot be determined from reviews.
2. **`14352377997` reveals a second, separable problem**: a customer who bought *lifetime* Premium is now being asked to *subscribe*. That is a model-migration issue, not an activation bug, and it produced a "DO NOT BUY IT" on a public storefront.
3. **Nobody in this cluster received a visible response.** No reviewer reports contact, and the two 4★ reviewers both asked for "빠른 조치" (fast action).

## 5.6 Barriers to upgrading — people who wanted to pay and did not

**36 reviewers (4.62%, very strong) express purchase intent or ask a purchase question.** The things that stop them, in order of frequency:

**(a) Price — 20 reviewers (2.57%), 14 of them non-Korean.** §3.3.7, §6.4 and §6.7. The clearest formulation is Japanese: *"300円くらいなら購入するつもりでしたが、1600円はちと高いので残念ながら断念！"* / "I was going to buy at around ¥300, but ¥1,600 is a bit steep so I'm reluctantly giving up" (`8685490868`) — and that was *before* the rise to ¥4,500.

**(b) No trial — 3 reviewers ask for one directly**, and the logic is always the same: the free tier is too constrained to evaluate the paid one. *"무료 사용해보고 마음이 생겨야 유료로 바꿀 마음이 생기잖아요"* / "you only get the urge to go paid after actually trying it free" (`12572214309`); *"프리미엄 기능이 잘와닿지 않아서 기간체험으로 만들어도 괜찮을 것 같습니다"* / "the Premium features don't land for me — a time-limited trial would be fine" (`8301510290`); *"무료 체험 2주 후 할인된 가격에 결제 이런거라든가.. 이만원 이하까지는 걍 결제하겠는데 거의 3만원이네요"* / "how about a two-week trial then a discount — I'd just pay under ₩20,000 but this is nearly ₩30,000" (`10850332771`).

**(c) All-or-nothing bundling — 3 reviewers propose unbundling, on three continents.** *"I'd like to upgrade to premium, but I don't need all the features. I'd need only no-ads, and more groups, and more intervals. Would appreciate separating the features so I can pay for only the ones I want"* (`7028234235`, CA); *"段階的に、広告削除とグループの制限解除とバックアップ機能追加で2000円くらいのプランがあったら嬉しいです"* / "I'd be glad of a staged ~¥2,000 plan with ad removal, group unlocking and backup" (`11510979900`, JP); *"adding a monthly payment plan would be so useful. The lifetime premium plan is not at all affordable to a wider audience"* (`10639075443`, PL).

**(d) Reliability — 2 reviewers (`reliability_blocks_purchase`, 0.26%, weak, mean ★ 1.500) say the bugs are why they will not pay.** *"위젯 버그는 안 고쳐주시나요 … 피드백 수용이나 업데이트가 자주 안 되는 것 같아서 유료 결제가 고민됨"* / "aren't you going to fix the widget bug? You don't seem to take feedback or update often, so I'm hesitating about paying" (`13840622066`, KR, 2★, Mar 2026); *"위젯 오류가 너무 많네요 … 몇년째 쓰고있는데 무료 사용해보고 마음이 생겨야 유료로 바꿀 마음이 생기잖아요"* (`12572214309`, KR, 1★). **Small in count, but it is the only theme that states the causal link between §3.3.2 and revenue explicitly.**

**(e) The purchase itself failing — 3 reviewers.** *"There's an issue where you try to get premium but it had a connection issue"* (`8245723501`, AU, 3★, titled *"Would be fun if it allowed me to get premium"*); *"광고제거때문에 프리미엄 결제하고싶은데 계속해서 알 수 없는 오류라는 창과 함께 결제가 되지않습니다"* (`8235594886`, 1★); *"구매를 하고싶은데 업데이트중이라고 기다리래요"* / "I want to buy but it tells me to wait because it's updating" (`8254302702`).

**(f) Mis-taps that become refunds — 6 `accidental_purchase` reviewers (0.77%), all Korean, all 2019–2023.** *"프리미엄이 궁금해서 눌러서 보고 창을 닫으려고 확인 버튼을 눌렀는데 구매가 되어버렸어요"* / "I tapped to see what Premium was, pressed OK to close the sheet, and it bought it" (`6288381604`); *"앱내구매 광고제거 모르고 눌렀는데 환불해주세요"* (`3982059993`). **Interpretation: a purchase sheet that can be confirmed by a dismissal gesture generated six refund requests and at least two one-star reviews. All six predate 2024, which is consistent with it having been fixed.**

## 5.7 Competitive position as payers describe it

Nine payers (9.09% of the segment) also carry `comparative_best`. The pattern is that **payment follows a completed search, not a trial**: *"수많은 목표관리 앱을 직접 사용하고 비교한 결과"* → purchase; *"앱 여러개 써보다가 이걸로 선택하고 유료 구입했어요"* (`10489526756`); *"평생 써본 습관앱중 최고"* (`9062339832`); *"루틴체크하는 어플 필요했는데 … 어플 다운받고 둘러보다가 바로 영구결제했어요"* / "I needed a routine app … downloaded it, looked around, and bought the permanent version right away" (`9541183862`).

**Against that, one payer states they are considering leaving** (`11277951619`, KR, 5★, Premium): *"uxui가 너무 훌륭해서 프리미엄으로 결제하여 사용하고 있어요. 결제한 만큼 이 어플을 오래오래 사용하고 싶습니다. 그런데 유일한 불편함이 바로 저 3가지입니다 … 이것때문에 다른 어플로 갈아타려고 고민이 됩니다"* / "the UX/UI is so excellent that I paid for Premium. Having paid, I want to use it for a long time. But these three things are my only discomfort … because of them I'm considering switching to another app." **Their three asks are all widget layouts.** That is the whole business case for §3.3.2 in one review.

**Two storefronts clear the 50-review threshold: Korea and the United States.** Everything below §6.4 is explicitly limited evidence.

**The concentration is the finding.** At 77.66%, Korea is not a market segment in this corpus — it *is* the corpus. Every global percentage in Part 3 is approximately "what Korean reviewers said", diluted by 22% of everyone else.

## 6.2 Korea — n = 605 (77.66% of corpus), mean 4.225 · **fully eligible**

| `update_regression` | 53 | **8.76%** | **3.45%** | high-priority |
